### 74L95 - 4-bit parallel-access shift register

- **Part:** 74L95
- **Description:** 4-bit parallel-access shift register
- **Formula:** QA = stateA; QB = stateB; QC = stateC ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 22 (PDF page 28)

![74L95 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74L95.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74L95 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74L95.png) | ![74L95 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74L95.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 7 (MSI/LSI data sheets)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-89, 7-92 (PDF pages 88, 91) (figures for SN74L95 (J, N package))
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA = stateA` - Register stage A (QA is the stage the serial input enters).
  - `QB = stateB` - Register stage B (QA is the stage the serial input enters).
  - `QC = stateC` - Register stage C (QA is the stage the serial input enters).
  - `QD = stateD` - Register stage D (QA is the stage the serial input enters).
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on falling edge of CK1 (MODE low) or of CK2 (MODE high)
  - MODE high, CK2 high: hold
  - MODE high, falling edge of CK2: QA QB QC QD = A B C D (parallel load; CK1 is ignored, serial entry is inhibited)
  - Shift left = parallel load with QB wired to A, QC to B, QD to C, and serial data on D
  - MODE low, CK1 high: hold
  - MODE low, falling edge of CK1: QA = SER, QB = old QA, QC = old QB, QD = old QC (shift right; CK2 is ignored)
  - Changes on MODE should be made while both clocks are low; the function table also shows hold for MODE rising with CK2 L CK1 L, MODE falling with CK2 L CK1 L, MODE falling with CK2 L CK1 H, MODE rising with CK2 H CK1 L, and MODE rising with CK2 H CK1 H
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FA` (flip-flop): `FA = (MODE AND A) OR (NOT MODE AND SER)` on falling edge (H->L) of (MODE AND CK2) OR (NOT MODE AND CK1)
  - stored `FB` (flip-flop): `FB = (MODE AND B) OR (NOT MODE AND FA)` on falling edge (H->L) of (MODE AND CK2) OR (NOT MODE AND CK1)
  - stored `FC` (flip-flop): `FC = (MODE AND C) OR (NOT MODE AND FB)` on falling edge (H->L) of (MODE AND CK2) OR (NOT MODE AND CK1)
  - stored `FD` (flip-flop): `FD = (MODE AND D) OR (NOT MODE AND FC)` on falling edge (H->L) of (MODE AND CK2) OR (NOT MODE AND CK1)
  - `QA = FA` (totem-pole)
  - `QB = FB` (totem-pole)
  - `QC = FC` (totem-pole)
  - `QD = FD` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | SER | input | - |
| 2 | B | input | - |
| 3 | C | input | - |
| 4 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |
| 5 | D | input | - |
| 6 | MODE | input | text: mode control: high = parallel load / shift left on CK2, low = shift right on CK1 |
| 7 | CK1 | clock | edge: falling; text: clock 1, R-shift |
| 8 | CK2 | clock | edge: falling; text: clock 2, L-shift / load |
| 9 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 11 | GND | ground | - |
| 12 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | A | input | - |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CK1,CK2 | QA,QB,QC,QD | low to high | - | 115 | 200 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 50 pF, RL 4 kohm |
| CK1,CK2 | QA,QB,QC,QD | high to low | - | 125 | 200 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 50 pF, RL 4 kohm |

- **Timing limits:** pulse width CK1,CK2 minNs 200; setup (high-level data) SER,A,B,C,D to clock falling minNs 100; setup (low-level data) SER,A,B,C,D to clock falling minNs 120; hold SER,A,B,C,D to clock falling minNs 0; time to enable clock 1 MODE to CK1 minNs 225; time to enable clock 2 MODE to CK2 minNs 200; time to inhibit clock 1 MODE to CK1 minNs 100; time to inhibit clock 2 MODE to CK2 minNs 0; max clock frequency CK1,CK2 minMHz 3 typMHz 5
- **Output current:** low 3.6 mA, high -0.2 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Low-power TTL: IOL 3.6 mA, IOH -200 uA (SN74L95). The 74L95 has a different pinout from the 7495.
- **Notes:** MODE is drawn as an input: it chooses which clock and which data path is used; it does not switch the outputs off. The datasheet gives no minimum propagation delays; the path-delay function uses 0 ns for the minimum.

### 74100 - 8-bit bistable latches

- **Part:** 74100
- **Description:** 8-bit bistable latches
- **Formula:** 1Q1 = state1Q1; 1Q2 = state1Q2; 1Q3 = state1Q3 ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 23 (PDF page 29)

![74100 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74100.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, section 7 (MSI/LSI data sheets)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-113, 7-114 (PDF pages 112-113) (figures for SN74100 (J, N package))
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Q1 = state1Q1` - Latch 1Q1: follows 1D1 while 1G is high, holds while 1G is low.
  - `1Q2 = state1Q2` - Latch 1Q2: follows 1D2 while 1G is high, holds while 1G is low.
  - `1Q3 = state1Q3` - Latch 1Q3: follows 1D3 while 1G is high, holds while 1G is low.
  - `1Q4 = state1Q4` - Latch 1Q4: follows 1D4 while 1G is high, holds while 1G is low.
  - `2Q1 = state2Q1` - Latch 2Q1: follows 2D1 while 2G is high, holds while 2G is low.
  - `2Q2 = state2Q2` - Latch 2Q2: follows 2D2 while 2G is high, holds while 2G is low.
  - `2Q3 = state2Q3` - Latch 2Q3: follows 2D3 while 2G is high, holds while 2G is low.
  - `2Q4 = state2Q4` - Latch 2Q4: follows 2D4 while 2G is high, holds while 2G is low.
- **Stored state:** 8 bits, changes on level of 1G (latches 1Q1-1Q4) and 2G (latches 2Q1-2Q4)
  - G high: Q = D (transparent)
  - G low: Q holds the level it had before the high-to-low transition of G
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `L1Q1` (latch): `L1Q1 = 1D1` on while high: 1G
  - stored `L1Q2` (latch): `L1Q2 = 1D2` on while high: 1G
  - stored `L1Q3` (latch): `L1Q3 = 1D3` on while high: 1G
  - stored `L1Q4` (latch): `L1Q4 = 1D4` on while high: 1G
  - stored `L2Q1` (latch): `L2Q1 = 2D1` on while high: 2G
  - stored `L2Q2` (latch): `L2Q2 = 2D2` on while high: 2G
  - stored `L2Q3` (latch): `L2Q3 = 2D3` on while high: 2G
  - stored `L2Q4` (latch): `L2Q4 = 2D4` on while high: 2G
  - `1Q1 = L1Q1` (totem-pole)
  - `1Q2 = L1Q2` (totem-pole)
  - `1Q3 = L1Q3` (totem-pole)
  - `1Q4 = L1Q4` (totem-pole)
  - `2Q1 = L2Q1` (totem-pole)
  - `2Q2 = L2Q2` (totem-pole)
  - `2Q3 = L2Q3` (totem-pole)
  - `2Q4 = L2Q4` (totem-pole)

### 7497 - Synchronous 6-bit binary rate multiplier

- **Part:** 7497
- **Description:** Synchronous 6-bit binary rate multiplier
- **Formula:** Z = NOT( (CLOCK' AND STROBE') AND ( (F AND QA') OR (E AND QA AND QB') OR (D AND QA AND QB AND QC') OR (C AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD') OR (B AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE') OR (A AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE AND QF') ) ); Y = NOT(Z AND UNITY/CASCADE); ENABLE OUTPUT = NOT(ENABLE INPUT' AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE AND QF) (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 23 (PDF page 29)

![7497 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/7497.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![7497 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-7497.png) | ![7497 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-7497.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN5497, SN7497, bulletin DL-S 7611802 (revised October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-102 to 7-105 (PDF pages 101-104) (figures for SN7497)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Z = NOT( (CLOCK' AND STROBE') AND ( (F AND QA') OR (E AND QA AND QB') OR (D AND QA AND QB AND QC') OR (C AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD') OR (B AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE') OR (A AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE AND QF') ) )` - Z is a NOR of six rate gates. Each rate gate is open while CLOCK and STROBE are both low and the 6-bit counter is in the state that belongs to that rate bit; rate input F gives 32 pulses per 64 clocks, E 16, D 8, C 4, B 2, A 1. So with the counter enabled Z gives M low pulses per 64 clock pulses, M = 32F + 16E + 8D + 4C + 2B + A, f_out = M * f_in / 64. With all rate inputs low Z stays high. Gate inputs read from the functional block diagram (see unknown).
  - `Y = NOT(Z AND UNITY/CASCADE)` - Y is the complement of Z while UNITY/CASCADE is high (M high pulses per 64 clocks); a low UNITY/CASCADE forces Y high. Tying UNITY/CASCADE to CLOCK with STROBE high passes the clock inverted to Y.
  - `ENABLE OUTPUT = NOT(ENABLE INPUT' AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE AND QF)` - Goes low once per 64 clocks, when the counter is enabled and reaches all ones (state 63); used as the enable/strobe of the next stage when cascading. Function table gives 1 pulse per 64 clocks. The block diagram (book page 7-103, read at 600 dpi on 2026-11-23) shows exactly seven inputs to this gate: the enable line and QA..QF.
- **Stored state:** 6 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - CLEAR high: counter QA..QF = 0 at once (function table: Y low, Z high, enable output high; note B says the levels of clock and strobe can change Y and Z, and a low unity/cascade keeps Y high)
  - CLEAR low, ENABLE INPUT low: the 6-bit synchronous binary counter (QA least significant) counts up by one on each rising clock edge, 64 states
  - ENABLE INPUT high: the counter holds
  - STROBE high: rate gates inhibited, Z high
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `QA` (counter): `QA = QA XOR (ENABLE INPUT')` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR high
  - stored `QB` (counter): `QB = QB XOR (ENABLE INPUT' AND QA)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR high
  - stored `QC` (counter): `QC = QC XOR (ENABLE INPUT' AND QA AND QB)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR high
  - stored `QD` (counter): `QD = QD XOR (ENABLE INPUT' AND QA AND QB AND QC)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR high
  - stored `QE` (counter): `QE = QE XOR (ENABLE INPUT' AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR high
  - stored `QF` (counter): `QF = QF XOR (ENABLE INPUT' AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR high
  - `Z = NOT( (CLOCK' AND STROBE') AND ( (F AND QA') OR (E AND QA AND QB') OR (D AND QA AND QB AND QC') OR (C AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD') OR (B AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE') OR (A AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE AND QF') ) )` (totem-pole)
  - `Y = NOT(Z AND UNITY/CASCADE)` (totem-pole)
  - `ENABLE OUTPUT = NOT(ENABLE INPUT' AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD AND QE AND QF)` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | B | input | text: rate input, weight 2^1 |
| 2 | E | input | text: rate input, weight 2^4 |
| 3 | F | input | text: rate input, weight 2^5 |
| 4 | A | input | text: rate input, weight 2^0 |
| 5 | Z | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 6 | Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | ENABLE OUTPUT | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 10 | STROBE | enable | active: low; whenInactive: high; text: Low enables the rate decoding gates; high inhibits them, so Z stays high (and Y then follows only the unity/cascade input).; controls: 5, 6 |
| 11 | ENABLE INPUT | enable | active: low; whenInactive: hold; text: Low lets the counter count; high holds the counter. Also gates the enable output.; controls: 7 |
| 12 | UNITY/CASCADE | input | text: Second input of the Y gate. Low forces Y high. |
| 13 | CLEAR | input | active: high; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 14 | C | input | text: rate input, weight 2^2 |
| 15 | D | input | text: rate input, weight 2^3 |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |

| ENABLE INPUT | ENABLE OUTPUT | low to high | - | 13 | 20 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| ENABLE INPUT | ENABLE OUTPUT | high to low | - | 14 | 21 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| STROBE | Z | low to high | - | 12 | 18 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, N = 10, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** max clock frequency CLOCK minMHz 25 typMHz 32; pulse width CLOCK minNs 20; pulse width CLEAR minNs 15; setup ENABLE INPUT to CLOCK rising minNs 25; setup ENABLE INPUT to CLOCK falling edge of the previous clock pulse minNs 0 maxNs tw(clock) - 10; hold ENABLE INPUT to CLOCK rising minNs 0 maxNs tw(clock) - 10; hold ENABLE INPUT to CLOCK falling edge of the previous clock pulse minNs 20 maxNs tcp - 10
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.4 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Clock frequency 0 to 25 MHz (recommended). Clock input equivalent Req 2 kOhm nominal, other inputs 4 kOhm. Clock input low-level input current -3.2 mA max, other inputs -1.6 mA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Exact gate inputs of the six rate gates were read from the scanned functional block diagram (book page 7-103); the line-by-line wiring is not printed as text.
- **Notes:** Pin names follow the datasheet pin-out: B 1, E 2, F 3, A 4, Z 5, Y 6, ENABLE OUTPUT 7, GND 8, CLOCK 9, STROBE 10, ENABLE INPUT 11, UNITY/CASCADE 12, CLEAR 13, C 14, D 15, VCC 16. The recommended-conditions table column header for the 74 part reads 'SN7494' in the book; it is the SN7497 column (misprint in the source).

### 74107 - Dual J-K master-slave flip-flop

- **Part:** 74107
- **Description:** Dual J-K master-slave flip-flop
- **Formula:** 1Q = FF1; 1Q' = NOT FF1; 2Q = FF2 ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 24 (PDF page 30)

![74107 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74107.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74107 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74107.png) | ![74107 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74107.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI SN54107, SN54LS107A, SN74107, SN74LS107A Dual J-K flip-flops with clear, SDLS036 (December 1983, revised March 1988)](https://www.ti.com/lit/ds/symlink/sn54107.pdf) p. 1, 3, 4 (figures for SN74107)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Q = FF1` - Flip-flop output.
  - `1Q' = NOT FF1` - Complement output.
  - `2Q = FF2` - Flip-flop output.
  - `2Q' = NOT FF2` - Complement output.
- **Stored state:** 2 bits, changes on high-to-low transition at the end of a positive CLK pulse (master-slave)
  - CLR low: Q = 0, Q' = 1 at once, overriding clock and data
  - CLR high, positive CLK pulse: J=L,K=L hold; J=H,K=L Q=1; J=L,K=H Q=0; J=H,K=H toggle
  - The J-K data is loaded into the master while CLK is high and transferred to the slave and outputs on the high-to-low clock transition; J and K must be stable while CLK is high
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FF1` (flip-flop): `FF1 = (1J AND NOT FF1) OR (NOT (1K) AND FF1)` on falling edge (H->L) of 1CLK; clear by 1CLR low
  - stored `FF2` (flip-flop): `FF2 = (2J AND NOT FF2) OR (NOT (2K) AND FF2)` on falling edge (H->L) of 2CLK; clear by 2CLR low
  - `1Q = FF1` (totem-pole)
  - `1Q' = NOT FF1` (totem-pole)
  - `2Q = FF2` (totem-pole)
  - `2Q' = NOT FF2` (totem-pole)

### 74S112 - Dual J-K negative-edge-triggered flip-flop with preset and clear

- **Part:** 74S112
- **Description:** Dual J-K negative-edge-triggered flip-flop with preset and clear
- **Formula:** 1Q = FF1; 1Q' = NOT FF1; 2Q = FF2 ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 24 (PDF page 30)

![74S112 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74S112.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74S112 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74S112.png) | ![74S112 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74S112.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 5, 54/74 families pin assignments and function tables](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/05.pdf) p. book page 5-34 (PDF page 34) (figures for SN74S112); [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 6, Series 54S/74S flip-flops: recommended operating conditions, electrical and switching characteristics](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/06.pdf) p. book pages 6-58, 6-59 (PDF pages 58, 59) (figures for SN74S112)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Q = FF1` - Flip-flop 1 output.
  - `1Q' = NOT FF1` - Complement of flip-flop 1 (except both high while PRESET and CLEAR are both low).
  - `2Q = FF2` - Flip-flop 2 output.
  - `2Q' = NOT FF2` - Complement of flip-flop 2 (except both high while PRESET and CLEAR are both low).
- **Stored state:** 2 bits, changes on falling edge of each flip-flop's CK
  - PRESET low, CLEAR high: Q = 1, Q' = 0 at once
  - PRESET high, CLEAR low: Q = 0, Q' = 1 at once
  - PRESET low and CLEAR low: Q = 1 and Q' = 1 (not stable: does not persist when preset and clear return high)
  - PRESET high, CLEAR high, falling CLOCK edge: J=L,K=L hold; J=H,K=L Q=1; J=L,K=H Q=0; J=H,K=H toggle
  - CLOCK high (no falling edge): hold
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FF1` (flip-flop): `FF1 = (1J AND NOT FF1) OR (NOT (1K) AND FF1)` on falling edge (H->L) of 1CK; set by 1PR low; clear by 1CLR low; both: neither wins: Q and Q' are both high while both act; not stable, the bit left when both return high is not given
  - stored `FF2` (flip-flop): `FF2 = (2J AND NOT FF2) OR (NOT (2K) AND FF2)` on falling edge (H->L) of 2CK; set by 2PR low; clear by 2CLR low; both: neither wins: Q and Q' are both high while both act; not stable, the bit left when both return high is not given
  - `1Q = FF1 OR (1PR' AND 1CLR')` (totem-pole)
  - `1Q' = NOT FF1 OR (1PR' AND 1CLR')` (totem-pole)
  - `2Q = FF2 OR (2PR' AND 2CLR')` (totem-pole)
  - `2Q' = NOT FF2 OR (2PR' AND 2CLR')` (totem-pole)

|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1CK | clock | edge: falling |
| 2 | 1K | input | - |
| 3 | 1J | input | - |
| 4 | 1PR | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 5 | 1Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 6 | 1Q' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | 2Q' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | 2Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | 2PR | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 11 | 2J | input | - |
| 12 | 2K | input | - |
| 13 | 2CK | clock | edge: falling |
| 14 | 2CLR | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 15 | 1CLR | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PRESET or CLEAR | Q or Q' | low to high | - | 4 | 7 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 280 ohm |
| PRESET or CLEAR | Q' or Q | high to low | - | 5 | 7 | clock high; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 280 ohm |
| PRESET or CLEAR | Q' or Q | high to low | - | 5 | 7 | clock low; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 280 ohm |
| CLOCK | Q or Q' | low to high | - | 4 | 7 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 280 ohm |
| CLOCK | Q or Q' | high to low | - | 5 | 7 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 280 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** max clock frequency CLOCK minMHz 80 typMHz 125; pulse width CLOCK high minNs 6; pulse width CLOCK low minNs 6.5; pulse width PRESET or CLEAR low minNs 8; setup J, K (high-level data) to CLOCK falling minNs 3; setup J, K (low-level data) to CLOCK falling minNs 3; hold J, K to CLOCK falling minNs 0
- **Output current:** low 20 mA, high -1 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Clear and preset inputs: IIL -7 mA max (clear tested with preset high and preset with clear high), IIH 100 uA; J, K IIL -1.6 mA, IIH 50 uA; clock IIL -4 mA, IIH 100 uA.
- **Notes:** Data taken from the family tables of the 1976 TTL Data Book, columns 'S112.

- **Formulas:**

  - `1Q' = NOT FF1` - Complement of flip-flop 1 (except both high while PRESET and CLEAR are both low).
  - `2Q = FF2` - Flip-flop 2 output.

- **Stored state:** 2 bits, changes on falling edge of CK
  - PRESET low, CLEAR high: Q = 1, Q' = 0 at once
  - PRESET high, CLEAR low: Q = 0, Q' = 1 at once

  - stored `FF2` (flip-flop): `FF2 = (2J AND NOT FF2) OR (NOT (2K) AND FF2)` on falling edge (H->L) of CK; set by 2PR low; clear by CLR low; both: neither wins: Q and Q' are both high while both act; not stable, the bit left when both return high is not given

### 74120 - Pulse synchronizer

- **Part:** 74120
- **Description:** Pulse synchronizer
- **Formula:** Y = C AND PASS; Y' = NOT Y (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 25 (PDF page 31)

![74120 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74120.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74120 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74120.png) | ![74120 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74120.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54120, SN74120 Dual pulse synchronizers/drivers, bulletin DL-S 7211537 (revised December 1972)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-118 to 7-120 (PDF pages 117-119) (figures for SN74120)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Y = C AND PASS` - While the section is passing pulses, Y carries the clock pulses of C (16 ns typical from C); the start/stop latch and the clock latch decide whether a pulse is passed, and a pulse once started is passed in full (latched, not clipped). Formula is the behaviour described in the text, not the gate-level wiring.
  - `Y' = NOT Y` - Complement of Y (9 ns typical from C).
- **Stored state:** 2 bits, changes on levels and high-to-low transitions on R, S1, S2; clock C
  - R X, S1 L, S2 X: pass output pulses
  - R X, S1 X, S2 L: pass output pulses
  - R L, S1 H, S2 H: inhibit output pulses
  - R H, S1 high-to-low, S2 H: start output pulses
  - R H, S1 H, S2 high-to-low: start output pulses
  - R high-to-low, S1 H, S2 H: stop output pulses
  - R H, S1 H, S2 H: continue (operation started by the last high-to-low transition continues)
  - After start, M low: pulses keep passing until a stop command; M high: only a single clock pulse is passed
  - When stopped by S or R, the setup times decide which is the last pulse. A low-to-high transition on M meeting tsu(H) passes that positive clock pulse, then inhibits remaining pulses. Low-to-high transitions on M in the 20 ns after the falling edge of the clock pulse may or may not let the next pulse through.
  - Once an output pulse has started it is not cut short (C is latch-controlled)
- **Behaviour:** not written yet (see BUGS.md B3).

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1M | input | text: mode control: low passes a train of pulses, high passes a single pulse. Internal pull-up. |
| 2 | 1S1 | input | text: start/stop latch input (see function table). Internal pull-up. |
| 3 | 1S2 | input | text: start/stop latch input (see function table). Internal pull-up. |
| 4 | 1R | input | text: latch input; a high-to-low transition stops output pulses. Internal pull-up. |
| 5 | 1C | input | text: clock input whose pulses are passed to the outputs; no pull-up |
| 6 | 1Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | 1Y' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | 2Y' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | 2Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 11 | 2C | input | text: clock input, section 2 |
| 12 | 2R | input | text: Internal pull-up. |
| 13 | 2S1 | input | text: Internal pull-up. |
| 14 | 2S2 | input | text: Internal pull-up. |
| 15 | 2M | input | text: mode control, section 2. Internal pull-up. |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C | Y | low to high | - | 14 | 22 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 45 pF, RL 133 ohm |
| C | Y | high to low | - | 17 | 25 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 45 pF, RL 133 ohm |
| C | Y' | low to high | - | 10 | 16 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 45 pF, RL 133 ohm |
| C | Y' | high to low | - | 8 | 13 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 45 pF, RL 133 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** setup any input except M to C minNs 12; setup M to C minNs 0; setup M to C minNs 12; hold any input except M to C minNs 3; hold M to C minNs 20
- **Output current:** low 48 mA, high -2.4 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Clock driver: outputs drive 60 Series 54/74 loads high and 30 loads low. M, R and S inputs have internal pull-up resistors (15 kOhm nominal shown in the input schematic) and may be left open; the C input has none. C input low-level current -3.2 mA max, other inputs -2.1 mA max.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The exact clock edge each setup/hold time refers to is drawn in Figures 2-5 (book page 7-121), which were not read.; The resting levels of Y and Y' while pulses are inhibited are not stated in the text that was read.; Gate-level formula: the functional block diagram was read only as a picture; the formula given is the behaviour from the text and function table.
- **Notes:** Typical power 127 mW per driver.

### 74121 - Monostable multivibrator

- **Part:** 74121
- **Description:** Monostable multivibrator
- **Formula:** Q = PULSE; Q' = NOT PULSE (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 26 (PDF page 32)

![74121 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74121.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74121 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74121.png) | ![74121 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74121.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI SN54121, SN74121 Monostable multivibrators with Schmitt-trigger inputs, SDLS042A (May 1983, revised May 2005)](https://www.ti.com/lit/ds/symlink/sn74121.pdf) p. 1, 3, 4 (figures for SN74121)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Q = PULSE` - Q goes high for tw(out) after a trigger, then low. Trigger = a rising edge of T where T = B AND NOT(A1 AND A2): a falling edge on A1 or A2 while B is high and the other A is high, or a rising edge on B while A1 or A2 is low.
  - `Q' = NOT PULSE` - Complement of Q.
- **Stored state:** 1 bits, changes on rising edge of T = B AND (A1' OR A2')
  - Steady state: Q low, Q' high (function table rows with no trigger transition)
  - Trigger: Q goes high and Q' low for tw(out); once fired the outputs do not depend on further input transitions (not retriggerable)
  - Pulse width tw(out) = Cext * RT * ln 2, about 0.7 * Cext * RT (RT = Rext or Rint), within the jitter-free range of Cext 10 pF to 10 uF and RT 2 kOhm to 40 kOhm (SN74121)
  - With no external timing parts (Rint to VCC, Cext and Rext/Cext open) the pulse is typically 30 or 35 ns
  - Input pulses may be of any duration relative to the output pulse
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `P` (one-shot): pulse on rising edge (L->H) of B AND (NOT A1 OR NOT A2), tw = Cext RT ln2, about 0.7 Cext RT (RT = Rext or Rint); about 30-35 ns with no timing parts
  - `Q = P` (totem-pole)
  - `Q' = NOT P` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Q' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 2 | NC | nc | - |
| 3 | A1 | input | text: negative-transition trigger input |
| 4 | A2 | input | text: negative-transition trigger input |
| 5 | B | input | text: positive-transition trigger input (Schmitt), or inhibit; Schmitt input |
| 6 | Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | NC | nc | - |
| 9 | Rint | analog | text: internal timing resistor; connect to VCC to use it |
| 10 | Cext | analog | text: external timing capacitor, one side |
| 11 | Rext/Cext | analog | text: external timing capacitor other side and external timing resistor (to VCC) |
| 12 | NC | nc | - |
| 13 | NC | nc | - |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A1 or A2 | Q | low to high | - | 45 | 70 | Cext 80 pF, Rint to VCC; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| B | Q | low to high | - | 35 | 55 | Cext 80 pF, Rint to VCC; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| A1 or A2 | Q' | high to low | - | 50 | 80 | Cext 80 pF, Rint to VCC; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| B | Q' | high to low | - | 40 | 65 | Cext 80 pF, Rint to VCC; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** pulse width A1, A2, B (input pulse) minNs 50; output pulse width Q minNs 70 typNs 110 maxNs 150; output pulse width Q typNs 30 maxNs 50; output pulse width Q minNs 600 typNs 700 maxNs 800; output pulse width Q minNs 6000000 typNs 7000000 maxNs 8000000
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.4 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Schmitt input B: VT+ typ 1.55 V, max 2 V; VT- min 0.8 V, typ 1.35 V. Rext 1.4 to 40 kOhm (74), Cext 0 to 1000 uF. Duty cycle max 67% at RT 2 kOhm, 90% at RT = max Rext. Input pulse width min 50 ns. Rate of rise or fall: B min 1 V/s, A1/A2 min 1 V/us. B input IIL -3.2 mA, IIH 80 uA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The resistance of the internal timing resistor Rint is not stated on the pages read.
- **Notes:** Formula for the pulse width from the datasheet description: tw(out) = Cext RT ln2 = 0.7 Cext RT.

- **Formula:** 1Q = PULSE1; 1Q' = NOT PULSE1; 2Q = PULSE2 ... (datasheet)

|---|---|
| ![74123 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74123.png) | ![74123 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74123.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI SN54122, SN54123, SN54130, SN54LS122, SN54LS123, SN74122, SN74123, SN74130, SN74LS122, SN74LS123 Retriggerable monostable multivibrators, SDLS043 (December 1983, revised March 1988)](https://www.ti.com/lit/ds/symlink/sn74123.pdf) p. 1, 2, 4, 5, 7 (figures for SN74123)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Q = PULSE1` - High for tw after a trigger; trigger = rising edge of T where T = 1CLR AND 1A' AND 1B; low at once when 1CLR is low.
  - `1Q' = NOT PULSE1` - Complement of Q.
  - `2Q = PULSE2` - High for tw after a trigger; trigger = rising edge of T where T = 2CLR AND 2A' AND 2B; low at once when 2CLR is low.
  - `2Q' = NOT PULSE2` - Complement of Q.
- **Stored state:** 2 bits, changes on trigger edges on A, B or CLR (each section)
  - CLR low: Q = 0, Q' = 1 at once (overriding clear ends the pulse)
  - A high or B low (steady): no trigger; Q low, Q' high once any started pulse has ended
  - CLR high, A low, B rising: trigger
  - CLR high, A falling, B high: trigger
  - CLR rising, A low, B high: trigger
  - Retriggerable: a new trigger during the pulse restarts the timing, so the pulse ends tw after the last trigger (plus tPLH); retrigger pulses starting before 0.22 * Cext (pF) ns after the first trigger are ignored
  - Pulse width for Cext > 1000 pF: tw = K * RT * Cext * (1 + 0.7/RT), K = 0.28 for the '123, RT in kOhm, Cext in pF, tw in ns
  - With the protection diode of datasheet Figure 2 (electrolytic Cext or when CLR is used): tw = KD * RT * Cext * (1 + 0.7/RT), KD = 0.25 for the '123
  - For Cext <= 1000 pF the width is read from datasheet Figure 4 (graph); no formula
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `P1` (one-shot): pulse on rising edge (L->H) of NOT 1A AND 1B AND 1CLR, tw = K RT Cext (1 + 0.7/RT), K = 0.28, RT in kohm, Cext in pF, tw in ns (Cext > 1000 pF); restarts on each trigger; clear by 1CLR low
  - stored `P2` (one-shot): pulse on rising edge (L->H) of NOT 2A AND 2B AND 2CLR, tw = K RT Cext (1 + 0.7/RT), K = 0.28, RT in kohm, Cext in pF, tw in ns (Cext > 1000 pF); restarts on each trigger; clear by 2CLR low
  - `1Q = P1` (totem-pole)
  - `1Q' = NOT P1` (totem-pole)
  - `2Q = P2` (totem-pole)
  - `2Q' = NOT P2` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1A | input | text: active-low trigger input (falling edge triggers) |
| 2 | 1B | input | text: active-high trigger input (rising edge triggers) |
| 3 | 1CLR | input | active: low; text: overriding clear, ends the output pulse; a rising edge on CLR with A low and B high also triggers; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 4 | 1Q' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 5 | 2Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 6 | 2Cext | analog | text: external timing capacitor (to the Rext/Cext pin) |
| 7 | 2Rext/Cext | analog | text: external timing capacitor (positive side) and timing resistor RT to VCC |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | 2A | input | text: active-low trigger input (falling edge triggers) |
| 10 | 2B | input | text: active-high trigger input (rising edge triggers) |
| 11 | 2CLR | input | active: low; text: overriding clear, ends the output pulse; a rising edge on CLR with A low and B high also triggers; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 12 | 2Q' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | 1Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | 1Cext | analog | text: external timing capacitor (to the Rext/Cext pin) |
| 15 | 1Rext/Cext | analog | text: external timing capacitor (positive side) and timing resistor RT to VCC |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A | Q | low to high | - | 22 | 33 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, Cext 0, Rext 5 kOhm |
| B | Q | low to high | - | 19 | 28 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, Cext 0, Rext 5 kOhm |
| A | Q' | high to low | - | 30 | 40 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, Cext 0, Rext 5 kOhm |
| B | Q' | high to low | - | 27 | 36 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, Cext 0, Rext 5 kOhm |
| CLR | Q | high to low | - | 18 | 27 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, Cext 0, Rext 5 kOhm |
| CLR | Q' | low to high | - | 30 | 40 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, Cext 0, Rext 5 kOhm |

- **Timing limits:** pulse width inputs (tw) minNs 40; output pulse width Q typNs 45 maxNs 76; output pulse width Q minNs 2760 typNs 3030 maxNs 3370
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74123: Rext 5 to 50 kOhm, Cext no restriction, wiring capacitance at Rext/Cext max 50 pF, input pulse duration min 40 ns. Clear input IIL -3.2 mA, IIH 80 uA; data inputs -1.6 mA, 40 uA. The datasheet calls only the 'LS122/'LS123 B input Schmitt; the 74123 is not.
- **Notes:** The SN74LS123 has different figures (same datasheet, page 6); this file is the 74123.

- **Part:** 74132
- **Description:** Quadruple 2-input positive NAND Schmitt trigger
- **Formula:** 1Y = NOT(1A AND 1B); 2Y = NOT(2A AND 2B); 3Y = NOT(3A AND 3B) ... (datasheet)

(1976), section 5, pin assignments, 132](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/05.pdf) p. book page 5-37 (PDF page 37) (figures for SN74132); [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 6, Schmitt-trigger positive-NAND gates and inverters with totem-pole outputs, column '14, '132](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/06.pdf) p. book pages 6-14, 6-15 (PDF pages 14, 15) (figures for SN74132)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y = NOT(1A AND 1B)` - Positive-logic NAND with Schmitt-trigger (hysteresis) inputs.
  - `2Y = NOT(2A AND 2B)` - Positive-logic NAND with Schmitt-trigger (hysteresis) inputs.
  - `3Y = NOT(3A AND 3B)` - Positive-logic NAND with Schmitt-trigger (hysteresis) inputs.
  - `4Y = NOT(4A AND 4B)` - Positive-logic NAND with Schmitt-trigger (hysteresis) inputs.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):

  - `2Y = NOT(2A AND 2B)` (totem-pole)

  - `4Y = NOT(4A AND 4B)` (totem-pole)

### 74148 - 8-line-to-3-line priority encoder (equal to 9318)

- **Part:** 74148
- **Description:** 8-line-to-3-line priority encoder (equal to 9318)
- **Formula:** A2 = NOT(EI' AND (4' OR 5' OR 6' OR 7')); A1 = NOT(EI' AND (7' OR 6' OR (5 AND 4 AND 3') OR (5 AND 4 AND 2'))); A0 = NOT(EI' AND (7' OR (6 AND 5') OR (6 AND 4 AND 3') OR (6 AND 4 AND 2 AND 1'))) ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 28 (PDF page 34)

![74148 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74148.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74148 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74148.png) | ![74148 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74148.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54147, SN54148, SN54LS147, SN54LS148, SN74147, SN74148 (TIM9907), SN74LS147, SN74LS148, bulletin DL-S 7611727 (October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-151, 7-153, 7-154 (PDF pages 150, 152, 153) (figures for SN74148)
- **Formulas:**
  - `A2 = NOT(EI' AND (4' OR 5' OR 6' OR 7'))` - Low when enabled and any of inputs 4-7 is low.
  - `A1 = NOT(EI' AND (7' OR 6' OR (5 AND 4 AND 3') OR (5 AND 4 AND 2')))` - Inverted bit 1 of the number of the highest-numbered low input.
  - `A0 = NOT(EI' AND (7' OR (6 AND 5') OR (6 AND 4 AND 3') OR (6 AND 4 AND 2 AND 1')))` - Inverted bit 0 of the number of the highest-numbered low input.
  - `GS = NOT(EI' AND (0' OR 1' OR 2' OR 3' OR 4' OR 5' OR 6' OR 7'))` - Low when enabled and at least one data input is low.
  - `EO = NOT(EI' AND 0 AND 1 AND 2 AND 3 AND 4 AND 5 AND 6 AND 7)` - Low when enabled and no data input is low; feeds EI of the next lower-priority stage.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `A2 = NOT(EI' AND (4' OR 5' OR 6' OR 7'))` (totem-pole) - drives when EI low, else 1
  - `A1 = NOT(EI' AND (7' OR 6' OR (5 AND 4 AND 3') OR (5 AND 4 AND 2')))` (totem-pole) - drives when EI low, else 1
  - `A0 = NOT(EI' AND (7' OR (6 AND 5') OR (6 AND 4 AND 3') OR (6 AND 4 AND 2 AND 1')))` (totem-pole) - drives when EI low, else 1
  - `GS = NOT(EI' AND (0' OR 1' OR 2' OR 3' OR 4' OR 5' OR 6' OR 7'))` (totem-pole) - drives when EI low, else 1
  - `EO = NOT(EI' AND 0 AND 1 AND 2 AND 3 AND 4 AND 5 AND 6 AND 7)` (totem-pole) - drives when EI low, else 1

| 13 | 3 | input | text: data input, active low |

- **Formulas:**
  - `W = NOT(STROBE' AND ((D' AND C' AND B' AND A' AND E0) OR (D' AND C' AND B' AND A AND E1) OR (D' AND C' AND B AND A' AND E2) OR (D' AND C' AND B AND A AND E3) OR (D' AND C AND B' AND A' AND E4) OR (D' AND C AND B' AND A AND E5) OR (D' AND C AND B AND A' AND E6) OR (D' AND C AND B AND A AND E7) OR (D AND C' AND B' AND A' AND E8) OR (D AND C' AND B' AND A AND E9) OR (D AND C' AND B AND A' AND E10) OR (D AND C' AND B AND A AND E11) OR (D AND C AND B' AND A' AND E12) OR (D AND C AND B' AND A AND E13) OR (D AND C AND B AND A' AND E14) OR (D AND C AND B AND A AND E15)))` - With STROBE low, W is the complement of the data input E0-E15 picked by D,C,B,A (D most significant). - **Part:** 74151
- **Description:** Data selector/multiplexer 8-1
- **Formula:** Y = STROBE' AND ((C' AND B' AND A' AND D0) OR (C' AND B' AND A AND D1) OR (C' AND B AND A' AND D2) OR (C' AND B AND A AND D3) OR (C AND B' AND A' AND D4) OR (C AND B' AND A AND D5) OR (C AND B AND A' AND D6) OR (C AND B AND A AND D7)); W = NOT Y (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 29 (PDF page 35)

![74151 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74151.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74151 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74151.png) | ![74151 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74151.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54150, SN54151A, SN54152A, SN54LS151, SN54LS152, SN54S151, SN74150, SN74151A, SN74LS151, SN74S151 Data selectors/multiplexers, bulletin DL-S 7611819 (revised October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-157 to 7-160 (PDF pages 156-159) (figures for SN74151A)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Y = STROBE' AND ((C' AND B' AND A' AND D0) OR (C' AND B' AND A AND D1) OR (C' AND B AND A' AND D2) OR (C' AND B AND A AND D3) OR (C AND B' AND A' AND D4) OR (C AND B' AND A AND D5) OR (C AND B AND A' AND D6) OR (C AND B AND A AND D7))` - With STROBE low, Y follows the data input D0-D7 picked by C,B,A. STROBE high forces Y low.
  - `W = NOT Y` - Complement of Y; STROBE high forces W high.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `Y = STROBE' AND ((C' AND B' AND A' AND D0) OR (C' AND B' AND A AND D1) OR (C' AND B AND A' AND D2) OR (C' AND B AND A AND D3) OR (C AND B' AND A' AND D4) OR (C AND B' AND A AND D5) OR (C AND B AND A' AND D6) OR (C AND B AND A AND D7))` (totem-pole)
  - `W = NOT Y` (totem-pole)

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The datasheet read is for the SN74151A (the 'A' version); the original SN74151 without suffix was not found as a separate datasheet.
- **Notes:** whenInactive for STROBE is not one of the single words in FORMAT.md: the two outputs go to different levels (Y low, W high). The '151A has address buffers with symmetrical delays through the complementary paths.

### 74153 - Dual 4-line-to-1-line data selector/multiplexer

- **Part:** 74153
- **Description:** Dual 4-line-to-1-line data selector/multiplexer
- **Formula:** 1Y = 1G' AND ((B'A' AND 1C0) OR (B'A AND 1C1) OR (BA' AND 1C2) OR (BA AND 1C3)); 2Y = 2G' AND ((B'A' AND 2C0) OR (B'A AND 2C1) OR (BA' AND 2C2) OR (BA AND 2C3)) (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 29 (PDF page 35)

![74153 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74153.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74153 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74153.png) | ![74153 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74153.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54153 ... SN74153, SN74L153, SN74LS153, SN74S153, bulletin DL-S 7611852 (revised October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-165, 7-167 (PDF pages 164, 166) (figures for SN74153)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y = 1G' AND ((B'A' AND 1C0) OR (B'A AND 1C1) OR (BA' AND 1C2) OR (BA AND 1C3))` - When 1G is low, 1Y follows the 1C input picked by B,A; when 1G is high, 1Y is low.
  - `2Y = 2G' AND ((B'A' AND 2C0) OR (B'A AND 2C1) OR (BA' AND 2C2) OR (BA AND 2C3))` - When 2G is low, 2Y follows the 2C input picked by B,A; when 2G is high, 2Y is low.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `1Y = 1G' AND ((B'A' AND 1C0) OR (B'A AND 1C1) OR (BA' AND 1C2) OR (BA AND 1C3))` (totem-pole) - drives when 1G low, else 0
  - `2Y = 2G' AND ((B'A' AND 2C0) OR (B'A AND 2C1) OR (BA' AND 2C2) OR (BA AND 2C3))` (totem-pole) - drives when 2G low, else 0

### 74154 - 4-line-to-16-line decoder/demultiplexer

- **Part:** 74154
- **Description:** 4-line-to-16-line decoder/demultiplexer
- **Formula:** 0 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C' AND B' AND A'); 1 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C' AND B' AND A); 2 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C' AND B AND A') ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 30 (PDF page 36)

![74154 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74154.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74154 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74154.png) | ![74154 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74154.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54154, SN54L154, SN74154, SN74L154, bulletin DL-S 7211805 (December 1972)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-171 to 7-173 (PDF pages 170-172) (figures for SN74154)
- **Formulas:**
  - `0 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C' AND B' AND A')` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 0.
  - `1 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C' AND B' AND A)` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 1.
  - `2 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C' AND B AND A')` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 2.
  - `3 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C' AND B AND A)` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 3.
  - `4 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C AND B' AND A')` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 4.
  - `5 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C AND B' AND A)` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 5.
  - `6 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C AND B AND A')` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 6.
  - `7 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C AND B AND A)` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 7.
  - `8 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C' AND B' AND A')` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 8.
  - `9 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C' AND B' AND A)` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 9.
  - `10 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C' AND B AND A')` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 10.
  - `11 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C' AND B AND A)` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 11.
  - `12 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C AND B' AND A')` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 12.
  - `13 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C AND B' AND A)` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 13.
  - `14 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C AND B AND A')` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 14.
  - `15 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C AND B AND A)` - Low only when G1 and G2 are both low and D,C,B,A = 15.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `0 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C' AND B' AND A')` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1
  - `1 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C' AND B' AND A)` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1
  - `2 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C' AND B AND A')` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1
  - `3 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C' AND B AND A)` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1
  - `4 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C AND B' AND A')` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1
  - `5 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C AND B' AND A)` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1
  - `6 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C AND B AND A')` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1
  - `7 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D' AND C AND B AND A)` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1
  - `8 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C' AND B' AND A')` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1
  - `9 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C' AND B' AND A)` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1
  - `10 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C' AND B AND A')` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1
  - `11 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C' AND B AND A)` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1
  - `12 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C AND B' AND A')` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1
  - `13 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C AND B' AND A)` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1
  - `14 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C AND B AND A')` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1
  - `15 = NOT(G1' AND G2' AND D AND C AND B AND A)` (totem-pole) - drives when G1 low and G2 low, else 1

### 74155 - Dual 2-line-to-4-line decoder/demultiplexer (74156 is equal)

- **Part:** 74155
- **Description:** Dual 2-line-to-4-line decoder/demultiplexer (74156 is equal)
- **Formula:** 1Y0 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND B'A'); 1Y1 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND B'A); 1Y2 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND BA') ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 30 (PDF page 36)

![74155 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74155.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74155 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74155.png) | ![74155 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74155.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54155, SN54156, SN54LS155, SN54LS156, SN74155, SN74156, SN74LS155, SN74LS156, bulletin DL-S 7611850 (revised October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-175 to 7-177 (PDF pages 174-176) (figures for SN74155)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y0 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND B'A')` - Low when 1G is low, 1C is high and B,A = 0.
  - `1Y1 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND B'A)` - Low when 1G is low, 1C is high and B,A = 1.
  - `1Y2 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND BA')` - Low when 1G is low, 1C is high and B,A = 2.
  - `1Y3 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND BA)` - Low when 1G is low, 1C is high and B,A = 3.
  - `2Y0 = NOT(2G' AND 2C' AND B'A')` - Low when 2G is low, 2C is low and B,A = 0.
  - `2Y1 = NOT(2G' AND 2C' AND B'A)` - Low when 2G is low, 2C is low and B,A = 1.
  - `2Y2 = NOT(2G' AND 2C' AND BA')` - Low when 2G is low, 2C is low and B,A = 2.
  - `2Y3 = NOT(2G' AND 2C' AND BA)` - Low when 2G is low, 2C is low and B,A = 3.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `1Y0 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND B'A')` (totem-pole) - drives when 1G low, else 1
  - `1Y1 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND B'A)` (totem-pole) - drives when 1G low, else 1
  - `1Y2 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND BA')` (totem-pole) - drives when 1G low, else 1
  - `1Y3 = NOT(1G' AND 1C AND BA)` (totem-pole) - drives when 1G low, else 1
  - `2Y0 = NOT(2G' AND 2C' AND B'A')` (totem-pole) - drives when 2C low and 2G low, else 1
  - `2Y1 = NOT(2G' AND 2C' AND B'A)` (totem-pole) - drives when 2C low and 2G low, else 1
  - `2Y2 = NOT(2G' AND 2C' AND BA')` (totem-pole) - drives when 2C low and 2G low, else 1
  - `2Y3 = NOT(2G' AND 2C' AND BA)` (totem-pole) - drives when 2C low and 2G low, else 1

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| 1C | Y | high to low | - | 20 | 30 | 3 levels of logic; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** 74155 has totem-pole outputs; the 74156 is the open-collector version (its electrical figures were not read).
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** 74156 (open-collector) electrical and switching figures were not read; this file describes the 74155.
- **Notes:** With 1C tied to 2C and 1G tied to 2G it is a 3-line-to-8-line decoder, C (1C/2C) the most significant select: C low selects section 2, C high section 1 (function table).

SN74157, SN74L157, SN74LS157, SN74LS158, SN74S157, SN74S158, bulletin DL-S 7611847 (revised October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-181, 7-184 (PDF pages 180, 183) (figures for SN74157)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y = STROBE' AND ((SELECT' AND 1A) OR (SELECT AND 1B))` - 1Y follows 1A when SELECT is low and 1B when SELECT is high; STROBE high forces it low.
  - `2Y = STROBE' AND ((SELECT' AND 2A) OR (SELECT AND 2B))` - 2Y follows 2A when SELECT is low and 2B when SELECT is high; STROBE high forces it low.
  - `3Y = STROBE' AND ((SELECT' AND 3A) OR (SELECT AND 3B))` - 3Y follows 3A when SELECT is low and 3B when SELECT is high; STROBE high forces it low.
  - `4Y = STROBE' AND ((SELECT' AND 4A) OR (SELECT AND 4B))` - 4Y follows 4A when SELECT is low and 4B when SELECT is high; STROBE high forces it low.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `1Y = STROBE' AND ((SELECT' AND 1A) OR (SELECT AND 1B))` (totem-pole) - drives when STROBE low, else 0
  - `2Y = STROBE' AND ((SELECT' AND 2A) OR (SELECT AND 2B))` (totem-pole) - drives when STROBE low, else 0
  - `3Y = STROBE' AND ((SELECT' AND 3A) OR (SELECT AND 3B))` (totem-pole) - drives when STROBE low, else 0
  - `4Y = STROBE' AND ((SELECT' AND 4A) OR (SELECT AND 4B))` (totem-pole) - drives when STROBE low, else 0

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | SELECT | input | text: low selects the A inputs, high the B inputs |
| 2 | 1A | input | - |
| 3 | 1B | input | - |
| 4 | 1Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 5 | 2A | input | - |
| 6 | 2B | input | - |
| 7 | 2Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | 3Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | 3B | input | - |
| 11 | 3A | input | - |
| 12 | 4Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | 4B | input | - |
| 14 | 4A | input | - |
| 15 | STROBE | enable | active: low; whenInactive: low; controls: 4, 7, 9, 12 |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| Data | Y | high to low | - | 9 | 14 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| Strobe | Y | low to high | - | 13 | 20 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| Strobe | Y | high to low | - | 14 | 21 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| Select | Y | low to high | - | 15 | 23 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| Select | Y | high to low | - | 18 | 27 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** The datasheet's '158 types are only 'LS158 and 'S158 (inverted outputs; with STROBE high their outputs are high); no plain 74158 is listed.
- **Notes:** This file describes the 74157 (true outputs).

### 74161 - Synchronous 4-bit counter

- **Part:** 74161
- **Description:** Synchronous 4-bit counter
- **Formula:** QA,QB,QC,QD = counter state bits 0..3; RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT = ENABLE T AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 31 (PDF page 37)

![74161 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74161.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74161 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74161.png) | ![74161 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74161.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54160 thru SN54163 ... SN74160 thru SN74163 Synchronous 4-bit counters, bulletin DL-S 7611385 (October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-190, 7-196, 7-197 (PDF pages 189, 195, 196) (figures for SN74161)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA,QB,QC,QD = counter state bits 0..3` - QA is the least significant bit.
  - `RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT = ENABLE T AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD` - High at count 15 while ENABLE T is high; the pulse lasts about as long as the high part of QA. Used to enable the next stage.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - CLEAR low: QA-QD = 0 at once, regardless of clock, load and enable inputs
  - else LOAD low: QA,QB,QC,QD = A,B,C,D at the next rising clock edge, regardless of the enable inputs
  - else ENABLE P and ENABLE T both high: count up by one (binary, 0-15, wraps to 0) at each rising clock edge
  - else (P or T low): hold
  - Low-to-high transitions at LOAD should be avoided while the clock is low if the enable inputs are high at or before the transition
  - High-to-low transitions at ENABLE P or T should occur only while the clock is high
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FA` (counter): `FA = (NOT LOAD AND A) OR (LOAD AND (FA XOR (ENABLE P AND ENABLE T)))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FB` (counter): `FB = (NOT LOAD AND B) OR (LOAD AND (FB XOR (ENABLE P AND ENABLE T AND FA)))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FC` (counter): `FC = (NOT LOAD AND C) OR (LOAD AND (FC XOR (ENABLE P AND ENABLE T AND FA AND FB)))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FD` (counter): `FD = (NOT LOAD AND D) OR (LOAD AND (FD XOR (ENABLE P AND ENABLE T AND FA AND FB AND FC)))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - `QA = FA` (totem-pole)
  - `QB = FB` (totem-pole)
  - `QC = FC` (totem-pole)
  - `QD = FD` (totem-pole)
  - `RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT = ENABLE T AND FA AND FB AND FC AND FD` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | CLEAR | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 2 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 3 | A | input | - |
| 4 | B | input | - |
| 5 | C | input | - |
| 6 | D | input | - |
| 7 | ENABLE P | enable | active: high; whenInactive: hold; controls: 14, 13, 12, 11 |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | LOAD | input | active: low; text: synchronous load: acts at the next rising clock edge |
| 10 | ENABLE T | enable | active: high; whenInactive: hold; text: also gates the ripple carry output; when low, the counter holds and RIPPLE CARRY is low; controls: 14, 13, 12, 11, 15 |
| 11 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 15 | RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLOCK | RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT | low to high | - | 23 | 35 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT | high to low | - | 23 | 35 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | any Q | low to high | - | 13 | 20 | LOAD high; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | any Q | high to low | - | 15 | 23 | LOAD high; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | any Q | low to high | - | 17 | 25 | LOAD low; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | any Q | high to low | - | 19 | 29 | LOAD low; VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| ENABLE T | RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT | low to high | - | 11 | 16 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| ENABLE T | RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT | high to low | - | 11 | 16 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLEAR | any Q | high to low | - | 26 | 38 | clear delay measured from the clear input ('161); VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** max clock frequency CLOCK minMHz 25 typMHz 32; pulse width CLOCK minNs 25; pulse width CLEAR minNs 20; setup A, B, C, D to CLOCK minNs 20; setup ENABLE P to CLOCK minNs 20; setup LOAD to CLOCK minNs 25; hold any input to CLOCK minNs 0
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Clock and ENABLE T inputs are double loads: IIL -3.2 mA, IIH 80 uA; other inputs -1.6 mA, 40 uA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The ripple-carry formula is from the description text (T fed forward, high-level overflow pulse at the maximum count); the logic diagram page was not read gate by gate.
- **Notes:** Binary counter with direct (asynchronous) clear.

### 74163 - Fully synchronous binary counter

- **Part:** 74163
- **Description:** Fully synchronous binary counter
- **Formula:** QA,QB,QC,QD = counter state bits 0..3; RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT = ENABLE T AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 32 (PDF page 38)

![74163 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74163.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74163 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74163.png) | ![74163 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74163.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54160 thru SN54163 ... SN74160 thru SN74163 Synchronous 4-bit counters, bulletin DL-S 7611385 (October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-190, 7-196, 7-197 (PDF pages 189, 195, 196) (figures for SN74163)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA,QB,QC,QD = counter state bits 0..3` - QA is the least significant bit.
  - `RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT = ENABLE T AND QA AND QB AND QC AND QD` - High at count 15 while ENABLE T is high; the pulse lasts about as long as the high part of QA. Used to enable the next stage.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - CLEAR low: QA-QD = 0 at the next rising clock edge, regardless of load and enable inputs
  - else LOAD low: QA,QB,QC,QD = A,B,C,D at the next rising clock edge, regardless of the enable inputs
  - else ENABLE P and ENABLE T both high: count up by one (binary, 0-15, wraps to 0) at each rising clock edge
  - else (P or T low): hold
  - Low-to-high transitions at LOAD should be avoided while the clock is low if the enable inputs are high at or before the transition
  - Low-to-high transitions at CLEAR should be avoided while the clock is low if the enable and load inputs are high at or before the transition
  - High-to-low transitions at ENABLE P or T should occur only while the clock is high
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FA` (counter): `FA = CLEAR AND ((NOT LOAD AND A) OR (LOAD AND (FA XOR (ENABLE P AND ENABLE T))))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK
  - stored `FB` (counter): `FB = CLEAR AND ((NOT LOAD AND B) OR (LOAD AND (FB XOR (ENABLE P AND ENABLE T AND FA))))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK
  - stored `FC` (counter): `FC = CLEAR AND ((NOT LOAD AND C) OR (LOAD AND (FC XOR (ENABLE P AND ENABLE T AND FA AND FB))))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK
  - stored `FD` (counter): `FD = CLEAR AND ((NOT LOAD AND D) OR (LOAD AND (FD XOR (ENABLE P AND ENABLE T AND FA AND FB AND FC))))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK
  - `QA = FA` (totem-pole)
  - `QB = FB` (totem-pole)
  - `QC = FC` (totem-pole)
  - `QD = FD` (totem-pole)
  - `RIPPLE CARRY OUTPUT = ENABLE T AND FA AND FB AND FC AND FD` (totem-pole)

### 74164 - 8-bit shift register

- **Part:** 74164
- **Description:** 8-bit shift register
- **Formula:** QA..QH = register bits 0..7 (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 32 (PDF page 38)

![74164 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74164.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74164 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74164.png) | ![74164 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74164.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54164, SN54L164, SN54LS164, SN74164, SN74L164, SN74LS164 8-bit parallel-out serial shift registers, bulletin DL-S 7611835 (revised October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-206, 7-208 (PDF pages 205, 207) (figures for SN74164)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA..QH = register bits 0..7` - Parallel outputs of the 8 stages; QA is the first stage.
- **Stored state:** 8 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - CLEAR low: QA..QH = 0 at once
  - CLEAR high, CLOCK low: hold
  - CLEAR high, rising CLOCK edge: QA = A AND B; QB..QH = QA..QG before the edge (one-bit shift)
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FA` (flip-flop): `FA = A AND B` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FB` (flip-flop): `FB = FA` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FC` (flip-flop): `FC = FB` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FD` (flip-flop): `FD = FC` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FE` (flip-flop): `FE = FD` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FF` (flip-flop): `FF = FE` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FG` (flip-flop): `FG = FF` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FH` (flip-flop): `FH = FG` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - `QA = FA` (totem-pole)
  - `QB = FB` (totem-pole)
  - `QC = FC` (totem-pole)
  - `QD = FD` (totem-pole)
  - `QE = FE` (totem-pole)
  - `QF = FF` (totem-pole)
  - `QG = FG` (totem-pole)
  - `QH = FH` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | A | input | text: gated serial input |
| 2 | B | input | text: gated serial input |
| 3 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 4 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 5 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 6 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 9 | CLEAR | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 10 | QE | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 11 | QF | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | QG | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | QH | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLEAR | any Q | high to low | - | 24 | 36 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 800 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| CLEAR | any Q | high to low | - | 28 | 42 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 800 ohm, CL 50 pF |
| CLOCK | any Q | low to high | 8 | 17 | 27 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 800 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| CLOCK | any Q | low to high | 10 | 20 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 800 ohm, CL 50 pF |
| CLOCK | any Q | high to low | 10 | 21 | 32 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 800 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| CLOCK | any Q | high to low | 10 | 25 | 37 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 800 ohm, CL 50 pF |

- **Timing limits:** max clock frequency CLOCK minMHz 25 typMHz 36; pulse width CLOCK or CLEAR minNs 20; setup A, B to CLOCK minNs 15; hold A, B to CLOCK minNs 5
- **Output current:** low 8 mA, high -0.4 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Note the low output current: IOL 8 mA, IOH -400 uA (half a standard 74 output).
- **Notes:** A low at either serial input makes the next bit shifted in a low; data at the serial inputs may change while the clock is high or low, only data meeting the setup time is taken.

### 74165 - 8-bit shift register

- **Part:** 74165
- **Description:** 8-bit shift register
- **Formula:** QH = register bit H (last stage); QH' = NOT QH (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 33 (PDF page 39)

![74165 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74165.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74165 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74165.png) | ![74165 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74165.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54165, SN54LS165, SN74165, SN74LS165 Parallel-load 8-bit shift registers, bulletin DL-S 7611375 (October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-212, 7-214 (PDF pages 211, 213) (figures for SN74165)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QH = register bit H (last stage)` - Output of the eighth stage.
  - `QH' = NOT QH` - Complement of the eighth stage.
- **Stored state:** 8 bits, changes on rising edge of (CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT) while SHIFT/LOAD is high
  - SHIFT/LOAD low: QA..QH = A..H at once (direct overriding load)
  - SHIFT/LOAD high, CLOCK INHIBIT low, CLOCK low: hold
  - SHIFT/LOAD high, CLOCK INHIBIT low, rising CLOCK edge: QA = SERIAL INPUT, QB..QH = QA..QG before the edge (shift toward QH)
  - SHIFT/LOAD high, CLOCK INHIBIT high: hold
  - The clock is a 2-input NOR of CLOCK and CLOCK INHIBIT; holding either high inhibits clocking, holding either low lets the other be the clock
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FA` (flip-flop): `FA = SERIAL INPUT` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; at once `A` while `NOT SHIFT/LOAD`
  - stored `FB` (flip-flop): `FB = FA` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; at once `B` while `NOT SHIFT/LOAD`
  - stored `FC` (flip-flop): `FC = FB` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; at once `C` while `NOT SHIFT/LOAD`
  - stored `FD` (flip-flop): `FD = FC` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; at once `D` while `NOT SHIFT/LOAD`
  - stored `FE` (flip-flop): `FE = FD` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; at once `E` while `NOT SHIFT/LOAD`
  - stored `FF` (flip-flop): `FF = FE` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; at once `F` while `NOT SHIFT/LOAD`
  - stored `FG` (flip-flop): `FG = FF` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; at once `G` while `NOT SHIFT/LOAD`
  - stored `FH` (flip-flop): `FH = FG` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; at once `H` while `NOT SHIFT/LOAD`
  - `QH = FH` (totem-pole)
  - `QH' = NOT FH` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | SHIFT/LOAD | input | active: low; text: low loads A..H into the register directly, independent of clock, clock inhibit and serial input; high allows shifting; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 2 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising; text: NORed with CLOCK INHIBIT; either may be used as the clock |
| 3 | E | input | text: parallel data input |
| 4 | F | input | text: parallel data input |
| 5 | G | input | text: parallel data input |
| 6 | H | input | text: parallel data input |
| 7 | QH' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | QH | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | SERIAL INPUT | input | - |
| 11 | A | input | text: parallel data input |
| 12 | B | input | text: parallel data input |
| 13 | C | input | text: parallel data input |
| 14 | D | input | text: parallel data input |
| 15 | CLOCK INHIBIT | input | active: high; text: high inhibits clocking; change it to high only while CLOCK is high |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
