### 74166 - 8-bit shift register

- **Part:** 74166
- **Description:** 8-bit shift register
- **Formula:** QH = register bit H (last stage) (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 33 (PDF page 39)

![74166 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74166.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74166 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74166.png) | ![74166 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74166.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54166, SN54LS166, SN74166, SN74LS166 8-bit shift registers, bulletin DL-S 7611808 (October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-217, 7-219 (PDF pages 216, 218) (figures for SN74166)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QH = register bit H (last stage)` - Serial output of the eighth stage.
- **Stored state:** 8 bits, changes on rising edge of (CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT)
  - CLEAR low: all eight flip-flops = 0 at once, overriding all other inputs including the clock
  - CLEAR high, SHIFT/LOAD low, clock edge: QA..QH = A..H (synchronous parallel load; serial flow inhibited)
  - CLEAR high, SHIFT/LOAD high, clock edge: QA = SERIAL INPUT, QB..QH = QA..QG before the edge
  - No clock edge (CLOCK or CLOCK INHIBIT held high, or clock steady): hold
  - The clock is a 2-input NOR of CLOCK and CLOCK INHIBIT, clocked on the low-to-high edge of the clock pulse
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FA` (flip-flop): `FA = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD AND A) OR (SHIFT/LOAD AND SERIAL INPUT)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FB` (flip-flop): `FB = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD AND B) OR (SHIFT/LOAD AND FA)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FC` (flip-flop): `FC = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD AND C) OR (SHIFT/LOAD AND FB)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FD` (flip-flop): `FD = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD AND D) OR (SHIFT/LOAD AND FC)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FE` (flip-flop): `FE = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD AND E) OR (SHIFT/LOAD AND FD)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FF` (flip-flop): `FF = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD AND F) OR (SHIFT/LOAD AND FE)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FG` (flip-flop): `FG = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD AND G) OR (SHIFT/LOAD AND FF)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FH` (flip-flop): `FH = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD AND H) OR (SHIFT/LOAD AND FG)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR low
  - `QH = FH` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | SERIAL INPUT | input | - |
| 2 | A | input | text: parallel data input |
| 3 | B | input | text: parallel data input |
| 4 | C | input | text: parallel data input |
| 5 | D | input | text: parallel data input |
| 6 | CLOCK INHIBIT | input | active: high; text: high inhibits clocking; change it to high only while CLOCK is high |
| 7 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising; text: NORed with CLOCK INHIBIT |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | CLEAR | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 10 | E | input | text: parallel data input |
| 11 | F | input | text: parallel data input |
| 12 | G | input | text: parallel data input |
| 13 | QH | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | H | input | text: parallel data input |
| 15 | SHIFT/LOAD | input | text: high: serial shift; low: synchronous parallel load at the next clock edge |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLEAR | QH | high to low | - | 23 | 35 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | QH | high to low | - | 20 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | QH | low to high | - | 17 | 26 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** max clock frequency CLOCK minMHz 25 typMHz 35; pulse width CLOCK or CLEAR minNs 20; setup SHIFT/LOAD to CLOCK minNs 30; setup data (A..H, SERIAL INPUT) to CLOCK minNs 20; hold any input to CLOCK minNs 0
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** All inputs buffered to one standard load.

### 74170 - 4-by-4 register file

- **Part:** 74170
- **Description:** 4-by-4 register file
- **Formula:** Q1 = NOT(GR') OR ((RB'RA' AND W0B1) OR (RB'RA AND W1B1) OR (RBRA' AND W2B1) OR (RBRA AND W3B1)); Q2 = NOT(GR') OR ((RB'RA' AND W0B2) OR (RB'RA AND W1B2) OR (RBRA' AND W2B2) OR (RBRA AND W3B2)); Q3 = NOT(GR') OR ((RB'RA' AND W0B3) OR (RB'RA AND W1B3) OR (RBRA' AND W2B3) OR (RBRA AND W3B3)) ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 34 (PDF page 40)

![74170 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74170.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74170 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74170.png) | ![74170 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74170.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54170, SN54LS170, SN74170, SN74LS170 4-by-4 register files with open-collector outputs, bulletin DL-S 7611349 (revised October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-237, 7-238, 7-240, 7-241 (PDF pages 236, 237, 239, 240) (figures for SN74170)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Q1 = NOT(GR') OR ((RB'RA' AND W0B1) OR (RB'RA AND W1B1) OR (RBRA' AND W2B1) OR (RBRA AND W3B1))` - With GR low, Q1 shows bit 1 of the word picked by RB,RA (true data, non-destructive read); with GR high the output is off (high with a pull-up).
  - `Q2 = NOT(GR') OR ((RB'RA' AND W0B2) OR (RB'RA AND W1B2) OR (RBRA' AND W2B2) OR (RBRA AND W3B2))` - With GR low, Q2 shows bit 2 of the word picked by RB,RA (true data, non-destructive read); with GR high the output is off (high with a pull-up).
  - `Q3 = NOT(GR') OR ((RB'RA' AND W0B3) OR (RB'RA AND W1B3) OR (RBRA' AND W2B3) OR (RBRA AND W3B3))` - With GR low, Q3 shows bit 3 of the word picked by RB,RA (true data, non-destructive read); with GR high the output is off (high with a pull-up).
  - `Q4 = NOT(GR') OR ((RB'RA' AND W0B4) OR (RB'RA AND W1B4) OR (RBRA' AND W2B4) OR (RBRA AND W3B4))` - With GR low, Q4 shows bit 4 of the word picked by RB,RA (true data, non-destructive read); with GR high the output is off (high with a pull-up).
- **Stored state:** 16 bits, changes on level of GW (transparent latches)
  - GW low, WB,WA = 0: word 0 latches = D1..D4 (follow the data inputs); other words hold
  - GW low, WB,WA = 1: word 1 = D; GW low, WB,WA = 2: word 2 = D; GW low, WB,WA = 3: word 3 = D
  - GW high: all words hold; data inputs inhibited
  - Writing and reading may happen at the same time at different addresses; a read of a word just written needs the latch time tlatch (25 ns) after the write
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - memory `W`: 4 words x 4 bits, address RA, RB
  - `Q1 = GR OR W1` (open-collector)
  - `Q2 = GR OR W2` (open-collector)
  - `Q3 = GR OR W3` (open-collector)
  - `Q4 = GR OR W4` (open-collector)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | D2 | input | - |
| 2 | D3 | input | - |
| 3 | D4 | input | - |
| 4 | RB | input | text: read address, most significant |
| 5 | RA | input | text: read address, least significant |
| 6 | Q4 | output | outputType: open-collector |
| 7 | Q3 | output | outputType: open-collector |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | Q2 | output | outputType: open-collector |
| 10 | Q1 | output | outputType: open-collector |
| 11 | GR | enable | active: low; whenInactive: high; text: read enable; high: outputs off (open collector, pulled high); controls: 10, 9, 7, 6 |
| 12 | GW | input | active: low; text: write enable: while low, the addressed word's latches follow D1-D4 (level, not edge) |
| 13 | WB | input | text: write address, most significant |
| 14 | WA | input | text: write address, least significant |
| 15 | D1 | input | - |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GR | any Q | low to high | - | 10 | 15 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| GR | any Q | high to low | - | 20 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| RA, RB | any Q | low to high | - | 23 | 35 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| RA, RB | any Q | high to low | - | 30 | 40 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| GW | any Q | low to high | - | 25 | 40 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| GW | any Q | high to low | - | 34 | 45 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| D | any Q | low to high | - | 20 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| D | any Q | high to low | - | 30 | 45 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** pulse width GW or GR minNs 25; setup D to GW minNs 10; setup WA, WB to GW minNs 15; hold D to GW minNs 15; hold WA, WB to GW minNs 5; latch time D to GW minNs 25
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high - mA; output may be pulled up to 5.5 V
- **Electrical notes:** Open-collector outputs: recommended VOH max 5.5 V; IOH (off-state) 30 uA max at VOH 5.5 V. Up to 256 outputs may be wire-AND connected.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Which GW edge the setup and hold times refer to is in datasheet Figure 2 (not read); from the description it is the end (rising edge) of the write-enable pulse, but that was not read from the figure.
- **Notes:** outputHighMaxCurrentMa is null because the outputs are open collector (no IOH source rating).

### 74173 - 4-bit D-type register with tri-state outputs

- **Part:** 74173
- **Description:** 4-bit D-type register with tri-state outputs
- **Formula:** 1Q = (M' AND N') ? FF1 : Z; 2Q = (M' AND N') ? FF2 : Z; 3Q = (M' AND N') ? FF3 : Z ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 34 (PDF page 40)

![74173 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74173.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74173 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74173.png) | ![74173 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74173.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54173, SN54LS173, SN74173, SN74LS173 4-bit D-type registers with 3-state outputs, bulletin DL-S 7611721 (October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-249, 7-251 (PDF pages 248, 250) (figures for SN74173)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Q = (M' AND N') ? FF1 : Z` - Shows flip-flop 1 when M and N are both low; high impedance when either is high.
  - `2Q = (M' AND N') ? FF2 : Z` - Shows flip-flop 2 when M and N are both low; high impedance when either is high.
  - `3Q = (M' AND N') ? FF3 : Z` - Shows flip-flop 3 when M and N are both low; high impedance when either is high.
  - `4Q = (M' AND N') ? FF4 : Z` - Shows flip-flop 4 when M and N are both low; high impedance when either is high.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - CLEAR high: all four flip-flops = 0 at once
  - CLEAR low, CLOCK low (no edge): hold
  - CLEAR low, rising CLOCK, G1 high or G2 high: hold
  - CLEAR low, rising CLOCK, G1 low and G2 low: flip-flop = D
  - The output controls M and N do not affect the flip-flops
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FF1` (flip-flop): `FF1 = ((G1 OR G2) AND FF1) OR (G1' AND G2' AND 1D)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR high
  - stored `FF2` (flip-flop): `FF2 = ((G1 OR G2) AND FF2) OR (G1' AND G2' AND 2D)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR high
  - stored `FF3` (flip-flop): `FF3 = ((G1 OR G2) AND FF3) OR (G1' AND G2' AND 3D)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR high
  - stored `FF4` (flip-flop): `FF4 = ((G1 OR G2) AND FF4) OR (G1' AND G2' AND 4D)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR high
  - `1Q = FF1` (three-state) - drives when M low and N low, else Z
  - `2Q = FF2` (three-state) - drives when M low and N low, else Z
  - `3Q = FF3` (three-state) - drives when M low and N low, else Z
  - `4Q = FF4` (three-state) - drives when M low and N low, else Z

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | M | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; text: output control; M or N high puts the outputs in the high-impedance state; the flip-flops keep working; controls: 3, 4, 5, 6 |
| 2 | N | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; controls: 3, 4, 5, 6 |
| 3 | 1Q | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ['1', '2'] |
| 4 | 2Q | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ['1', '2'] |
| 5 | 3Q | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ['1', '2'] |
| 6 | 4Q | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ['1', '2'] |
| 7 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | G1 | input | active: low; text: data enable: both G1 and G2 low loads D at the clock edge; either high holds |
| 10 | G2 | input | active: low; text: data enable (see G1) |
| 11 | 4D | input | - |
| 12 | 3D | input | - |
| 13 | 2D | input | - |
| 14 | 1D | input | - |
| 15 | CLEAR | input | active: high; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLEAR | any Q | high to low | - | 18 | 27 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 400 ohm, CL 50 pF |
| CLOCK | any Q | low to high | - | 28 | 43 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 400 ohm, CL 50 pF |
| CLOCK | any Q | high to low | - | 19 | 31 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 400 ohm, CL 50 pF |
| M or N | any Q | PZH | 7 | 16 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 400 ohm, CL 50 pF |
| M or N | any Q | PZL | 7 | 21 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 400 ohm, CL 50 pF |
| M or N | any Q | PHZ | 3 | 5 | 14 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 400 ohm, CL 5 pF |
| M or N | any Q | PLZ | 3 | 11 | 20 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 400 ohm, CL 5 pF |

- **Timing limits:** max clock frequency CLOCK minMHz 25 typMHz 35; pulse width CLOCK or CLEAR minNs 20; setup G1, G2 to CLOCK minNs 17; setup D to CLOCK minNs 10; setup CLEAR inactive state to CLOCK minNs 10; hold G1, G2 to CLOCK minNs 2; hold D to CLOCK minNs 10
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -5.2 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Three-state outputs; off-state current +/-40 uA. Up to 128 SN74173 outputs may share a bus and still drive two standard loads.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The switching table does not name the input for the enable/disable times; M or N is assumed from their role (output control). Marked as an assumption.
- **Notes:** Edges PZH, PZL, PHZ, PLZ are output enable/disable times (three-state), not PLH/PHL. All switching figures at RL 400 ohm.

(1976), section 7, TYPES SN54174, SN54175, SN54LS174, SN54LS175, SN54S174, SN54S175, SN74174, SN74175, SN74LS174, SN74LS175, SN74S174, SN74S175 Hex/quadruple D-type flip-flops with clear, bulletin DL-S 7611803 (revised October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-253, 7-256 (PDF pages 252, 255) (figures for SN74174)

### 74175 - Quadruple D-type flip-flop with clear

- **Part:** 74175
- **Description:** Quadruple D-type flip-flop with clear

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. (1976), section 7, TYPES SN54174, SN54175, SN54LS174, SN54LS175, SN54S174, SN54S175, SN74174, SN74175, SN74LS174, SN74LS175, SN74S174, SN74S175 Hex/quadruple D-type flip-flops with clear, bulletin DL-S 7611803 (revised October 1976)](http://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. book pages 7-253, 7-256 (PDF pages 252, 255) (figures for SN74175)

  - `2Q = FF2` - Flip-flop 2 output.
  - `2Q' = NOT FF2` - Complement of flip-flop 2.
  - `3Q = FF3` - Flip-flop 3 output.
  - `3Q' = NOT FF3` - Complement of flip-flop 3.
  - `4Q = FF4` - Flip-flop 4 output.
  - `4Q' = NOT FF4` - Complement of flip-flop 4.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - CLEAR low: Q = 0 (Q' = 1) at once
  - CLEAR high, rising CLOCK edge: Q = D
  - CLEAR high, CLOCK high or low (no edge): hold
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FF1` (flip-flop): `FF1 = 1D` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FF2` (flip-flop): `FF2 = 2D` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FF3` (flip-flop): `FF3 = 3D` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - stored `FF4` (flip-flop): `FF4 = 4D` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR low
  - `1Q = FF1` (totem-pole)
  - `1Q' = NOT FF1` (totem-pole)
  - `2Q = FF2` (totem-pole)
  - `2Q' = NOT FF2` (totem-pole)
  - `3Q = FF3` (totem-pole)
  - `3Q' = NOT FF3` (totem-pole)
  - `4Q = FF4` (totem-pole)
  - `4Q' = NOT FF4` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | CLEAR | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 2 | 1Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 3 | 1Q' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 4 | 1D | input | - |
| 5 | 2D | input | - |
| 6 | 2Q' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | 2Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 10 | 3Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 11 | 3Q' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | 3D | input | - |
| 13 | 4D | input | - |
| 14 | 4Q' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 15 | 4Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLEAR | any Q' | low to high | - | 16 | 25 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLEAR | any Q | high to low | - | 23 | 35 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | any output | low to high | - | 20 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | any output | high to low | - | 24 | 35 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** max clock frequency CLOCK minMHz 25 typMHz 35; pulse width CLOCK or CLEAR minNs 20; setup D to CLOCK minNs 20; setup CLEAR inactive state to CLOCK minNs 25; hold D to CLOCK minNs 5
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA

### 74181 - Arithmetic logic unit/function generator

- **Part:** 74181
- **Description:** Arithmetic logic unit/function generator
- **Formula:** F = function of A, B selected by S3..S0, M and Cn per Table 1 (active-low data) / Table 2 (active-high data), see state.rules; Cn+4 = carry out of the 4-bit arithmetic operation selected by S3..S0 with M low; P' = carry propagate of the 4-bit group (for the '182) ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 36 (PDF page 42)

![74181 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74181.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74181 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74181.png) | ![74181 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74181.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, SN54181/SN74181 (bulletin DL-S 7611831, Dec 1972, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-271 to 7-275 and 7-280 (PDF pages 270-274, 279) (figures for SN74181)
- **Formulas:**
  - `F = function of A, B selected by S3..S0, M and Cn per Table 1 (active-low data) / Table 2 (active-high data), see state.rules` - Each F output is one bit of the result chosen by the function table. The gate-level formula per bit is in the datasheet's functional block diagram (page 7-280) and was not transcribed.
  - `Cn+4 = carry out of the 4-bit arithmetic operation selected by S3..S0 with M low` - Ripple carry output. With active-high data (Table 2) a low at Cn+4' means carry. Used as a magnitude comparator in subtract mode, see the last rule in state.
  - `P' = carry propagate of the 4-bit group (for the '182)` - Group propagate output; feeds a P input of the SN74182.
  - `G' = carry generate of the 4-bit group (for the '182)` - Group generate output; feeds a G input of the SN74182.
  - `A=B = F0 AND F1 AND F2 AND F3` - Decoded from the function outputs (an AND of the four F lines in the block diagram); goes high (off) when all F outputs are high, i.e. A = B when the ALU is in subtract mode with Cn = H.
- **Stored state:** 0 bits, changes on combinational (no memory); this block holds the datasheet function tables
  - Table 1, ACTIVE-LOW DATA. Columns: S3 S2 S1 S0 \| M=H logic \| M=L, Cn=L (no carry) \| M=L, Cn=H (with carry)
  - L L L L \| F = A' \| F = A MINUS 1 \| F = A
  - L L L H \| F = (AB)' \| F = AB MINUS 1 \| F = AB
  - L L H L \| F = A' + B \| F = AB' MINUS 1 \| F = AB'
  - L L H H \| F = 1 \| F = MINUS 1 (2's COMP) \| F = ZERO
  - L H L L \| F = (A + B)' \| F = A PLUS (A + B') \| F = A PLUS (A + B') PLUS 1
  - L H L H \| F = B' \| F = AB PLUS (A + B') \| F = AB PLUS (A + B') PLUS 1
  - L H H L \| F = (A XOR B)' \| F = A MINUS B MINUS 1 \| F = A MINUS B
  - L H H H \| F = A + B' \| F = A + B' \| F = (A + B') PLUS 1
  - H L L L \| F = A'B \| F = A PLUS (A + B) \| F = A PLUS (A + B) PLUS 1
  - H L L H \| F = A XOR B \| F = A PLUS B \| F = A PLUS B PLUS 1
  - H L H L \| F = B \| F = AB' PLUS (A + B) \| F = AB' PLUS (A + B) PLUS 1
  - H L H H \| F = A + B \| F = (A + B) \| F = (A + B) PLUS 1
  - H H L L \| F = 0 \| F = A PLUS A (*) \| F = A PLUS A PLUS 1
  - H H L H \| F = AB' \| F = AB PLUS A \| F = AB PLUS A PLUS 1
  - H H H L \| F = AB \| F = AB' PLUS A \| F = AB' PLUS A PLUS 1
  - H H H H \| F = A \| F = A \| F = A PLUS 1
  - Table 2, ACTIVE-HIGH DATA. Columns: S3 S2 S1 S0 \| M=H logic \| M=L, Cn'=H (no carry) \| M=L, Cn'=L (with carry)
  - L L L L \| F = A' \| F = A \| F = A PLUS 1
  - L L L H \| F = (A + B)' \| F = A + B \| F = (A + B) PLUS 1
  - L L H L \| F = A'B \| F = A + B' \| F = (A + B') PLUS 1
  - L L H H \| F = 0 \| F = MINUS 1 (2's COMPL) \| F = ZERO
  - L H L L \| F = (AB)' \| F = A PLUS AB' \| F = A PLUS AB' PLUS 1
  - L H L H \| F = B' \| F = (A + B) PLUS AB' \| F = (A + B) PLUS AB' PLUS 1
  - L H H L \| F = A XOR B \| F = A MINUS B MINUS 1 \| F = A MINUS B
  - L H H H \| F = AB' \| F = AB' MINUS 1 \| F = AB'
  - H L L L \| F = A' + B \| F = A PLUS AB \| F = A PLUS AB PLUS 1
  - H L L H \| F = (A XOR B)' \| F = A PLUS B \| F = A PLUS B PLUS 1
  - H L H L \| F = B \| F = (A + B') PLUS AB \| F = (A + B') PLUS AB PLUS 1
  - H L H H \| F = AB \| F = AB MINUS 1 \| F = AB
  - H H L L \| F = 1 \| F = A PLUS A (*) \| F = A PLUS A PLUS 1
  - H H L H \| F = A + B' \| F = (A + B) PLUS A \| F = (A + B) PLUS A PLUS 1
  - H H H L \| F = A + B \| F = (A + B') PLUS A \| F = (A + B') PLUS A PLUS 1
  - H H H H \| F = A \| F = A MINUS 1 \| F = A
  - (*) Each bit is shifted to the next more significant position.
  - Comparator use (subtract mode S3..S0 = L,H,H,L): Cn=H, Cn+4=H -> A >= B (active-low data) / A <= B (active-high); Cn=H, Cn+4=L -> A < B / A > B; Cn=L, Cn+4=H -> A > B / A < B; Cn=L, Cn+4=L -> A <= B / A >= B
- **Behaviour:** not written yet (see BUGS.md B3).

- **Electrical notes:** SN74181: VCC 4.75-5.25 V; IOH -800 uA for all outputs except A=B; IOL 16 mA. A=B is open collector: IOH max 250 uA at VOH 5.5 V (the 5.5 V is that test condition; no separate pull-up limit is printed). Input loads: IIL mode -1.6 mA, any A or B -4.8, any S -6.4, carry -8 mA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Gate-level formulas for F0-F3, Cn+4, P' and G' were not transcribed from the functional block diagram (page 7-280); behaviour is given only by the function tables.; No separate maximum pull-up voltage for the open-collector A=B output beyond the 5.5 V IOH test condition.; No minimum propagation delays printed.
- **Notes:** Pin 2/1/23/... names are for active-low data (Table 1); with active-high data the same pins are A0, B0, ... F0..F3, Cn', Cn+4', X (pin 15), Y (pin 17) and Table 2 applies. Subtraction is 1's complement plus forced carry.

### 74182 - Look-ahead carry generator

- **Part:** 74182
- **Description:** Look-ahead carry generator
- **Formula:** Cn+x = G0 OR (P0 AND Cn); Cn+y = G1 OR (P1 AND G0) OR (P1 AND P0 AND Cn); Cn+z = G2 OR (P2 AND G1) OR (P2 AND P1 AND G0) OR (P2 AND P1 AND P0 AND Cn) ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 36 (PDF page 42)

![74182 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74182.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74182 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74182.png) | ![74182 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74182.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, SN54182/SN74182 (bulletin DL-S 7611823, Dec 1972, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-282 to 7-284 (PDF pages 281-283) (figures for SN74182)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Cn+x = G0 OR (P0 AND Cn)` - Datasheet equation, with G0 = NOT G0', P0 = NOT P0'. Function table: high when G0' low, or P0' low and Cn high; low otherwise.
  - `Cn+y = G1 OR (P1 AND G0) OR (P1 AND P0 AND Cn)` - Datasheet equation (G = NOT G', P = NOT P').
  - `Cn+z = G2 OR (P2 AND G1) OR (P2 AND P1 AND G0) OR (P2 AND P1 AND P0 AND Cn)` - Datasheet equation (G = NOT G', P = NOT P').
  - `G' = NOT (G3 OR (P3 AND G2) OR (P3 AND P2 AND G1) OR (P3 AND P2 AND P1 AND G0))` - Datasheet: G' = (G3 + P3G2 + P3P2G1 + P3P2P1G0)'. Low when the group generates a carry.
  - `P' = NOT (P3 AND P2 AND P1 AND P0)` - Low only when P3', P2', P1', P0' are all low.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `Cn+x = (NOT G0') OR ((NOT P0') AND Cn)` (totem-pole)
  - `Cn+y = (NOT G1') OR ((NOT P1') AND (NOT G0')) OR ((NOT P1') AND (NOT P0') AND Cn)` (totem-pole)
  - `Cn+z = (NOT G2') OR ((NOT P2') AND (NOT G1')) OR ((NOT P2') AND (NOT P1') AND (NOT G0')) OR ((NOT P2') AND (NOT P1') AND (NOT P0') AND Cn)` (totem-pole)
  - `G' = NOT ((NOT G3') OR ((NOT P3') AND (NOT G2')) OR ((NOT P3') AND (NOT P2') AND (NOT G1')) OR ((NOT P3') AND (NOT P2') AND (NOT P1') AND (NOT G0')))` (totem-pole)
  - `P' = NOT ((NOT P3') AND (NOT P2') AND (NOT P1') AND (NOT P0'))` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | G1' | input | text: carry generate input, active low (Y1) |
| 2 | P1' | input | text: carry propagate input, active low (X1) |
| 3 | G0' | input | text: carry generate input, active low (alternative name Y0) |
| 4 | P0' | input | text: carry propagate input, active low (X0) |
| 5 | G3' | input | text: carry generate input, active low (Y3) |
| 6 | P3' | input | text: carry propagate input, active low (X3) |
| 7 | P' | output | outputType: totem-pole; text: carry propagate output, active low (X) |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | Cn+z | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | G' | output | outputType: totem-pole; text: carry generate output, active low (Y) |
| 11 | Cn+y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | Cn+x | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | Cn | input | text: carry input |
| 14 | G2' | input | text: carry generate input, active low (Y2) |
| 15 | P2' | input | text: carry propagate input, active low (X2) |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| any | any | low to high | - | 11 | 17 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm; the datasheet gives one figure for all paths |
| any | any | high to low | - | 15 | 22 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm; the datasheet gives one figure for all paths |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74182 recommended: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, IOH -800 uA, IOL 16 mA, TA 0-70 C. Inputs load unequally: IIL Cn -3.2 mA, P3' -4.8, P2' -6.4, P0'/P1'/G3' -8, G0'/G2' -14.4, G1' -16 mA (several standard loads).
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** No minimum propagation delays printed (path-delay function uses 0 ns).
- **Notes:** The same circuit serves active-high data with the alternative names Y0-Y3, X0-X3, Cn', Y, X: Cn+x' = (Y0(X0 + Cn))' etc., as printed on page 7-282.

### 74191 - Synchronous up/down counter

- **Part:** 74191
- **Description:** Synchronous up/down counter
- **Formula:** QA..QD = Q[0..3]; MAX/MIN = (D/U' = L AND Q = 15) OR (D/U' = H AND Q = 0); RIPPLE CLOCK' = NOT (MAX/MIN AND G' = L AND CLOCK = L) (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 37 (PDF page 43)

![74191 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74191.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74191 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74191.png) | ![74191 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74191.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, SN54190/SN54191/SN74190/SN74191 (bulletin DL-S 7611865, Dec 1972, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-296, 7-297, 7-299 to 7-301 (PDF pages 295, 296, 298-300) (figures for SN74191)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA..QD = Q[0..3]` - The four counter bits (QA least significant).
  - `MAX/MIN = (D/U' = L AND Q = 15) OR (D/U' = H AND Q = 0)` - High-level pulse about one full clock cycle long when the counter overflows (counting up) or underflows (counting down); the timing diagram shows it high at 15 counting up and at 0 counting down.
  - `RIPPLE CLOCK' = NOT (MAX/MIN AND G' = L AND CLOCK = L)` - Low-level pulse equal in width to the low part of the clock when an overflow or underflow condition exists (datasheet wording); used to cascade into the next counter's enable or clock.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - LOAD' low: Q = D,C,B,A at once, independent of the clock (asynchronous)
  - G' high: counting inhibited, Q holds
  - G' low, D/U' low: Q = Q + 1 (mod 16) at the rising clock edge
  - G' low, D/U' high: Q = Q - 1 (mod 16) at the rising clock edge
  - Change G' and D/U' only while CLOCK is high
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FA` (counter): `FA = FA XOR (G' AND ((D/U' AND 1) OR (NOT D/U' AND 1)))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; at once `A` while `NOT LOAD'`
  - stored `FB` (counter): `FB = FB XOR (G' AND ((D/U' AND NOT FA) OR (NOT D/U' AND FA)))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; at once `B` while `NOT LOAD'`
  - stored `FC` (counter): `FC = FC XOR (G' AND ((D/U' AND NOT FA AND NOT FB) OR (NOT D/U' AND FA AND FB)))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; at once `C` while `NOT LOAD'`
  - stored `FD` (counter): `FD = FD XOR (G' AND ((D/U' AND NOT FA AND NOT FB AND NOT FC) OR (NOT D/U' AND FA AND FB AND FC)))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; at once `D` while `NOT LOAD'`
  - inside: `MAXMIN = (NOT D/U' AND FA AND FB AND FC AND FD) OR (D/U' AND NOT FA AND NOT FB AND NOT FC AND NOT FD)`
  - `QA = FA` (totem-pole)
  - `QB = FB` (totem-pole)
  - `QC = FC` (totem-pole)
  - `QD = FD` (totem-pole)
  - `MAX/MIN = MAXMIN` (totem-pole)
  - `RIPPLE CLOCK' = NOT (MAXMIN AND NOT G' AND NOT CLOCK)` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | B | input | text: data input (preset) |
| 2 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 3 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 4 | G' | input | text: count enable, active low: high inhibits counting; change it only while CLOCK is high |
| 5 | D/U' | input | text: down/up: low counts up, high counts down |
| 6 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | D | input | text: data input (preset) |
| 10 | C | input | text: data input (preset) |
| 11 | LOAD' | input | active: low; text: low: QA..QD take A..D at once, independent of the clock; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 12 | MAX/MIN | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | RIPPLE CLOCK' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 15 | A | input | text: data input (preset) |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LOAD' | QA..QD | low to high | - | 22 | 33 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| LOAD' | QA..QD | high to low | - | 33 | 50 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| A..D | QA..QD | low to high | - | 14 | 22 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| A..D | QA..QD | high to low | - | 35 | 50 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | RIPPLE CLOCK' | low to high | - | 13 | 20 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | RIPPLE CLOCK' | high to low | - | 16 | 24 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The exact gate logic of MAX/MIN and RIPPLE CLOCK' is read from the description and timing diagram, not transcribed from the block diagram.; Setup/hold reference edge for the data inputs is in datasheet figures 1 and 2, which were not read; recorded here as relative to LOAD'.; No minimum propagation delays printed.
- **Notes:** Master-slave flip-flops triggered on the low-to-high clock transition.

### 74193 - Up/down binary counter

- **Part:** 74193
- **Description:** Up/down binary counter
- **Formula:** QA..QD = Q[0..3]; CARRY' = NOT (Q = 15 AND UP = L); BORROW' = NOT (Q = 0 AND DOWN = L) (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 37 (PDF page 43)

![74193 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74193.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74193 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74193.png) | ![74193 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74193.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, SN54192/SN54193/SN74192/SN74193 (bulletin DL-S 7611828, Dec 1972, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-306, 7-311, 7-314 (PDF pages 305, 310, 313) (figures for SN74193)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA..QD = Q[0..3]` - The four counter bits (QA least significant).
  - `CARRY' = NOT (Q = 15 AND UP = L)` - Datasheet: the carry output produces a pulse equal in width to the count-up input when an overflow condition exists. Feeds the next counter's UP input.
  - `BORROW' = NOT (Q = 0 AND DOWN = L)` - Datasheet: the borrow output produces a pulse equal in width to the count-down input when the counter underflows. Feeds the next counter's DOWN input.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on rising edge of UP or DOWN
  - CLEAR high: Q = 0 at once, overrides count and load
  - LOAD' low (CLEAR low): Q = D,C,B,A at once
  - UP rising while DOWN high: Q = Q + 1 (mod 16)
  - DOWN rising while UP high: Q = Q - 1 (mod 16)
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FA` (counter): `FA = FA XOR ((DOWN AND 1) OR (UP AND 1))` on rising edge (L->H) of UP AND DOWN; at once `0` while `CLEAR`; at once `A` while `NOT LOAD'`
  - stored `FB` (counter): `FB = FB XOR ((DOWN AND FA) OR (UP AND NOT FA))` on rising edge (L->H) of UP AND DOWN; at once `0` while `CLEAR`; at once `B` while `NOT LOAD'`
  - stored `FC` (counter): `FC = FC XOR ((DOWN AND FA AND FB) OR (UP AND NOT FA AND NOT FB))` on rising edge (L->H) of UP AND DOWN; at once `0` while `CLEAR`; at once `C` while `NOT LOAD'`
  - stored `FD` (counter): `FD = FD XOR ((DOWN AND FA AND FB AND FC) OR (UP AND NOT FA AND NOT FB AND NOT FC))` on rising edge (L->H) of UP AND DOWN; at once `0` while `CLEAR`; at once `D` while `NOT LOAD'`
  - `QA = FA` (totem-pole)
  - `QB = FB` (totem-pole)
  - `QC = FC` (totem-pole)
  - `QD = FD` (totem-pole)
  - `CARRY' = NOT (FA AND FB AND FC AND FD AND NOT UP)` (totem-pole)
  - `BORROW' = NOT (NOT FA AND NOT FB AND NOT FC AND NOT FD AND NOT DOWN)` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | B | input | text: data input |
| 2 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 3 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 4 | DOWN | clock | edge: rising; text: count-down clock; the other count input must be high |
| 5 | UP | clock | edge: rising; text: count-up clock; the other count input must be high |
| 6 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | D | input | text: data input |
| 10 | C | input | text: data input |
| 11 | LOAD' | input | active: low; text: low: QA..QD take A..D, independent of the count pulses; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 12 | CARRY' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | BORROW' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | CLEAR | input | active: high; text: high forces all outputs low, independent of count and load; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 15 | A | input | text: data input |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UP | CARRY' | low to high | - | 17 | 26 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| UP | CARRY' | high to low | - | 16 | 24 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| DOWN | BORROW' | low to high | - | 16 | 24 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| DOWN | BORROW' | high to low | - | 16 | 24 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| UP or DOWN | QA..QD | low to high | - | 25 | 38 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| UP or DOWN | QA..QD | high to low | - | 31 | 47 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| LOAD' | QA..QD | low to high | - | 27 | 40 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| LOAD' | QA..QD | high to low | - | 29 | 40 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLEAR | QA..QD | high to low | - | 22 | 35 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** maxClockFrequency UP/DOWN minMHz 25 typMHz 32; pulseWidth any input minNs 20; setup A..D to LOAD' rising minNs 20; hold A..D to LOAD' rising minNs 0
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.4 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74193: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, IOH -400 uA, IOL 16 mA, TA 0-70 C.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** CARRY'/BORROW' formulas are from the description text, not transcribed from the logic diagram.; No minimum propagation delays printed.
- **Notes:** Master-slave flip-flops triggered by a low-to-high transition of either count input.

### 74195 - 4-bit parallel-access shift register

- **Part:** 74195
- **Description:** 4-bit parallel-access shift register
- **Formula:** QA..QD = Q[0..3]; QD' = NOT QD (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 38 (PDF page 44)

![74195 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74195.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74195 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74195.png) | ![74195 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74195.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, SN54195/SN74195 (bulletin DL-S 7611820, Mar 1974, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-324, 7-327 (PDF pages 323, 326) (figures for SN74195)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA..QD = Q[0..3]` - Register bits.
  - `QD' = NOT QD` - Complement output of the last stage.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - CLEAR' low: QA..QD = L, QD' = H at once
  - CLEAR' high, SHIFT/LOAD' low, clock rising: QA..QD = A,B,C,D (serial inputs ignored)
  - CLEAR' high, SHIFT/LOAD' high, CLOCK low: no change
  - Shift (SHIFT/LOAD' high, clock rising): QB=QAn, QC=QBn, QD=QCn, and QA from J,K':
  -   J=L, K'=H: QA = QAn (no change)
  -   J=L, K'=L: QA = L
  -   J=H, K'=H: QA = H
  -   J=H, K'=L: QA = NOT QAn (toggle)
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FA` (flip-flop): `FA = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD' AND A) OR (SHIFT/LOAD' AND ((J AND NOT FA) OR (K' AND FA)))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FB` (flip-flop): `FB = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD' AND B) OR (SHIFT/LOAD' AND (FA))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FC` (flip-flop): `FC = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD' AND C) OR (SHIFT/LOAD' AND (FB))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FD` (flip-flop): `FD = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD' AND D) OR (SHIFT/LOAD' AND (FC))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR' low
  - `QA = FA` (totem-pole)
  - `QB = FB` (totem-pole)
  - `QC = FC` (totem-pole)
  - `QD = FD` (totem-pole)
  - `QD' = NOT FD` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | CLEAR' | input | active: low; text: direct overriding clear; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 2 | J | input | text: serial input to first stage |
| 3 | K' | input | text: serial input to first stage (inverted K) |
| 4 | A | input | text: parallel data |
| 5 | B | input | text: parallel data |
| 6 | C | input | text: parallel data |
| 7 | D | input | text: parallel data |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | SHIFT/LOAD' | input | text: low: parallel load at the clock edge; high: shift QA toward QD |
| 10 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 11 | QD' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 15 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLEAR' | QA..QD | high to low | - | 19 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | QA..QD, QD' | low to high | - | 14 | 22 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | QA..QD, QD' | high to low | - | 17 | 26 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** maxClockFrequency CLOCK minMHz 30 typMHz 39; pulseWidth CLOCK minNs 16; pulseWidth CLEAR' minNs 12; setup SHIFT/LOAD' to CLOCK minNs 25; setup J, K', A..D to CLOCK minNs 20; setup CLEAR' inactive to CLOCK minNs 25; release SHIFT/LOAD' to CLOCK maxNs 10; hold J, K', A..D to CLOCK minNs 0
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74195: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, TA 0-70 C.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Clear to QD' (low-to-high) delay is not listed separately.; No minimum propagation delays printed.

### 74198 - 8-bit shift register

- **Part:** 74198
- **Description:** 8-bit shift register
- **Formula:** QA..QH = Q[0..7] (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 38 (PDF page 44)

![74198 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74198.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74198 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74198.png) | ![74198 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74198.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, SN54198/SN54199/SN74198/SN74199 (bulletin DL-S 7611841, Dec 1972, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-338, 7-339, 7-343, 7-344 (PDF pages 337, 338, 342, 343) (figures for SN74198)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA..QH = Q[0..7]` - Register bits.
- **Stored state:** 8 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - CLEAR' low: QA..QH = L at once
  - CLOCK low (no edge): hold
  - S1=H, S0=H: parallel load QA..QH = A..H at the clock edge
  - S1=L, S0=H: shift right (QA toward QH): QA = SR SER, QB = QAn, ..., QH = QGn
  - S1=H, S0=L: shift left (QH toward QA): QH = SL SER, QG = QHn, ..., QA = QBn
  - S1=L, S0=L: clock inhibited, hold
  - Change the mode inputs only while CLOCK is high
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FA` (flip-flop): `FA = (S1 AND S0 AND A) OR (NOT S1 AND S0 AND SR SER) OR (S1 AND NOT S0 AND FB) OR (NOT S1 AND NOT S0 AND FA)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FB` (flip-flop): `FB = (S1 AND S0 AND B) OR (NOT S1 AND S0 AND FA) OR (S1 AND NOT S0 AND FC) OR (NOT S1 AND NOT S0 AND FB)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FC` (flip-flop): `FC = (S1 AND S0 AND C) OR (NOT S1 AND S0 AND FB) OR (S1 AND NOT S0 AND FD) OR (NOT S1 AND NOT S0 AND FC)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FD` (flip-flop): `FD = (S1 AND S0 AND D) OR (NOT S1 AND S0 AND FC) OR (S1 AND NOT S0 AND FE) OR (NOT S1 AND NOT S0 AND FD)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FE` (flip-flop): `FE = (S1 AND S0 AND E) OR (NOT S1 AND S0 AND FD) OR (S1 AND NOT S0 AND FF) OR (NOT S1 AND NOT S0 AND FE)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FF` (flip-flop): `FF = (S1 AND S0 AND F) OR (NOT S1 AND S0 AND FE) OR (S1 AND NOT S0 AND FG) OR (NOT S1 AND NOT S0 AND FF)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FG` (flip-flop): `FG = (S1 AND S0 AND G) OR (NOT S1 AND S0 AND FF) OR (S1 AND NOT S0 AND FH) OR (NOT S1 AND NOT S0 AND FG)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FH` (flip-flop): `FH = (S1 AND S0 AND H) OR (NOT S1 AND S0 AND FG) OR (S1 AND NOT S0 AND SL SER) OR (NOT S1 AND NOT S0 AND FH)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR' low
  - `QA = FA` (totem-pole)
  - `QB = FB` (totem-pole)
  - `QC = FC` (totem-pole)
  - `QD = FD` (totem-pole)
  - `QE = FE` (totem-pole)
  - `QF = FF` (totem-pole)
  - `QG = FG` (totem-pole)
  - `QH = FH` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | S0 | input | text: mode control |
| 2 | SR SER | input | text: shift-right serial input |
| 3 | A | input | - |
| 4 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 5 | B | input | - |
| 6 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | C | input | - |
| 8 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 9 | D | input | - |
| 10 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 11 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 12 | GND | ground | - |
| 13 | CLEAR' | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 14 | QE | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 15 | E | input | - |
| 16 | QF | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 17 | F | input | - |
| 18 | QG | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 19 | G | input | - |
| 20 | QH | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 21 | H | input | - |
| 22 | SL SER | input | text: shift-left serial input |
| 23 | S1 | input | text: mode control; change S0/S1 only while CLOCK is high |
| 24 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLEAR' | QA..QH | high to low | - | 23 | 35 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | QA..QH | high to low | - | 20 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | QA..QH | low to high | - | 17 | 26 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** maxClockFrequency CLOCK minMHz 25 typMHz 35; pulseWidth CLOCK or CLEAR' minNs 20; setup S0, S1 to CLOCK minNs 30; setup data to CLOCK minNs 20; hold any input to CLOCK minNs 0
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74198/SN74199: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, TA 0-70 C.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** No minimum propagation delays printed.; Clear-to-output low-to-high is not applicable; no other paths listed.
- **Notes:** Bidirectional; four modes: load, shift right, shift left, inhibit.

### 74199 - 8-bit shift register

- **Part:** 74199
- **Description:** 8-bit shift register
- **Formula:** QA..QH = Q[0..7] (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 39 (PDF page 45)

![74199 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74199.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74199 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74199.png) | ![74199 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74199.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1976, SN54198/SN54199/SN74198/SN74199 (bulletin DL-S 7611841, Dec 1972, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-338, 7-339, 7-343, 7-344 (PDF pages 337, 338, 342, 343) (figures for SN74199)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA..QH = Q[0..7]` - Register bits.
- **Stored state:** 8 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK while CLOCK INHIBIT is low (the two inputs are gated together)
  - CLEAR' low: QA..QH = L at once
  - CLOCK INHIBIT high: hold
  - SHIFT/LOAD' low: QA..QH = A..H at the clock edge
  - SHIFT/LOAD' high: shift, QB..QH = QAn..QGn, and QA from J,K':
  -   J=L, K'=H: QA = QAn (no change)
  -   J=L, K'=L: QA = L
  -   J=H, K'=H: QA = H
  -   J=H, K'=L: QA = NOT QAn (toggle)
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FA` (flip-flop): `FA = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD' AND A) OR (SHIFT/LOAD' AND ((J AND NOT FA) OR (K' AND FA)))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FB` (flip-flop): `FB = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD' AND B) OR (SHIFT/LOAD' AND (FA))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FC` (flip-flop): `FC = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD' AND C) OR (SHIFT/LOAD' AND (FB))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FD` (flip-flop): `FD = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD' AND D) OR (SHIFT/LOAD' AND (FC))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FE` (flip-flop): `FE = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD' AND E) OR (SHIFT/LOAD' AND (FD))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FF` (flip-flop): `FF = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD' AND F) OR (SHIFT/LOAD' AND (FE))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FG` (flip-flop): `FG = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD' AND G) OR (SHIFT/LOAD' AND (FF))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR' low
  - stored `FH` (flip-flop): `FH = (NOT SHIFT/LOAD' AND H) OR (SHIFT/LOAD' AND (FG))` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK OR CLOCK INHIBIT; clear by CLEAR' low
  - `QA = FA` (totem-pole)
  - `QB = FB` (totem-pole)
  - `QC = FC` (totem-pole)
  - `QD = FD` (totem-pole)
  - `QE = FE` (totem-pole)
  - `QF = FF` (totem-pole)
  - `QG = FG` (totem-pole)
  - `QH = FH` (totem-pole)

| CLOCK | QA..QH | low to high | - | 17 | 26 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** maxClockFrequency CLOCK minMHz 25 typMHz 35; pulseWidth CLOCK or CLEAR' minNs 20; setup SHIFT/LOAD' to CLOCK minNs 30; setup data to CLOCK minNs 20; hold any input to CLOCK minNs 0
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74198/SN74199: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, TA 0-70 C.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** No minimum propagation delays printed.; Clear-to-output low-to-high is not applicable; no other paths listed.

### 74200 - 256 bit RAM

- **Part:** 74200
- **Description:** 256 bit RAM
- **Formula:** Y = MEM[address] when ME1'=ME2'=ME3'=L AND WE'=H; otherwise Z (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 39 (PDF page 45)

![74200 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74200.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74200 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74200.png) | ![74200 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74200.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1977 Memory Databook, DM54S200/DM74S200 256-bit read/write Schottky memories with TRI-STATE outputs](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1977_National_Memory_Databook.pdf) p. 2-11 to 2-13 (PDF pages 114-116) (figures for DM74S200 (Schottky); no datasheet for a plain 74200 was found)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Y = MEM[address] when ME1'=ME2'=ME3'=L AND WE'=H; otherwise Z` - Read: the stored bit (complement of the data that was written) appears at Y. Any ME' high, or WE' low (write), puts Y in high impedance.
- **Stored state:** 256 bits, changes on WE' low while ME1', ME2', ME3' are all low (level-sensitive write)
  - Write: ME' all low and WE' low: MEM[address] = NOT DATA (the complement is stored); output Z
  - Read: ME' all low and WE' high: Y = MEM[address]
  - Inhibit: any ME' high: Y = Z, no write
  - Address: row decoder A,B,C,H (pins 1,2,15,14) and column decoder D,E,F,G (pins 7,9,10,11), 16 x 16 matrix
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - memory `M`: 256 words x 1 bits, address A, B, C, H, D, E, F, G
  - `Y = NOT M1` (three-state) - drives when ME1' low and ME2' low and ME3' low and WE' high, else Z

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | A | input | text: address input |
| 2 | B | input | text: address input |
| 3 | ME1' | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; text: memory enable; all three must be low; controls: 6 |
| 4 | ME2' | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; controls: 6 |
| 5 | ME3' | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; controls: 6 |
| 6 | Y | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: 3,4,5,12; text: output: the stored bit, which is the complement of the data written |
| 7 | D | input | text: address input |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | E | input | text: address input |
| 10 | F | input | text: address input |
| 11 | G | input | text: address input |
| 12 | WE' | input | text: write enable, active low; while low the output is in high impedance |
| 13 | DATA | input | text: data input |
| 14 | H | input | text: address input |
| 15 | C | input | text: address input |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| address | Y | low to high | - | 33 | 50 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 30 pF, RL 300 ohm |
| address | Y | high to low | - | 29 | 50 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 30 pF, RL 300 ohm |
| ME' | Y | PZH | - | 21 | 35 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 30 pF, RL 300 ohm |
| ME' | Y | PZL | - | 10 | 20 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 30 pF, RL 300 ohm |
| WE' | Y | PZH | - | 24 | 40 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 30 pF, RL 300 ohm, sense recovery from write enable |
| WE' | Y | PZL | - | 12 | 40 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 30 pF, RL 300 ohm, sense recovery from write enable |
| ME' | Y | PHZ | - | 7 | 20 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 5 pF, RL 300 ohm |
| ME' | Y | PLZ | - | 20 | 35 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 5 pF, RL 300 ohm |
| WE' | Y | PHZ | - | 13 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 5 pF, RL 300 ohm |
| WE' | Y | PLZ | - | 16 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 5 pF, RL 300 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** pulseWidth WE' minNs 40; setup address to WE' minNs 0; setup DATA to WE' minNs 0; setup ME' to WE' minNs 0; hold address to WE' minNs 10; hold DATA to WE' minNs 10; hold ME' to WE' minNs 0
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -5.2 mA
- **Electrical notes:** DM74S200: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, TA 0-70 C, IOH -5.2 mA, IOL 16 mA, VOL max 0.45 V. PNP inputs: IIL max -250 uA (one-eighth of a 74S load). Off-state output current 50 / -50 uA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Datasheet is for the Schottky DM74S200; whether ND's '74200' is this part or an earlier non-Schottky part with the same pinout is not settled by any datasheet read.; No minimum delays printed.
- **Notes:** ND's catalogue id is 74200; the only datasheet found for a 256x1 RAM numbered 200 is the S (Schottky) version.

### 74221 - Dual monostable multivibrator (pin compatible with 74123)

- **Part:** 74221
- **Description:** Dual monostable multivibrator (pin compatible with 74123)
- **Formula:** Q = pulse state (high for tw after a trigger); Q' = NOT Q (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 40 (PDF page 46)

![74221 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74221.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74221 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74221.png) | ![74221 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74221.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book 2nd ed. 1976, SN54221/SN74221 (section 6, Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/06.pdf) p. 6-68 to 6-70 (PDF pages 68-70) (figures for SN74221)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Q = pulse state (high for tw after a trigger)` - Q goes high for tw = Cext * Rext * ln2 (about 0.7 Cext Rext) after a trigger; not retriggerable; once fired it ignores A and B.
  - `Q' = NOT Q` - Complement output.
- **Stored state:** 1 bits, changes on trigger edge on A or B, or rising edge of CLR'
  - CLR' low: Q = L, Q' = H at once, regardless of A and B
  - A high or B low (steady): no trigger, Q = L
  - CLR' high, A low, B rising: one output pulse
  - CLR' high, A falling, B high: one output pulse
  - CLR' rising, A low, B high: one output pulse
  - Pulse width tw(out) = Cext Rext ln2 ~ 0.7 Cext Rext; once fired the output no longer depends on A and B; not retriggerable
  - Cext = 0, Rext = 2 kohm gives typically 30 ns
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `P1` (one-shot): pulse on rising edge (L->H) of NOT 1A AND 1B AND 1CLR', tw = Cext Rext ln2, about 0.7 Cext Rext; Cext 0 and Rext 2 kohm give typically 30 ns; clear by 1CLR' low
  - stored `P2` (one-shot): pulse on rising edge (L->H) of NOT 2A AND 2B AND 2CLR', tw = Cext Rext ln2, about 0.7 Cext Rext; Cext 0 and Rext 2 kohm give typically 30 ns; clear by 2CLR' low
  - `1Q = P1` (totem-pole)
  - `1Q' = NOT P1` (totem-pole)
  - `2Q = P2` (totem-pole)
  - `2Q' = NOT P2` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1A | input | text: negative-transition trigger input (fires on a falling edge while B is high) |
| 2 | 1B | input | text: positive-transition Schmitt-trigger input (fires on a rising edge while A is low); Schmitt input |
| 3 | 1CLR' | input | active: low; text: overriding clear: low ends the pulse, Q low; a rising edge with A low and B high also fires; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 4 | 1Q' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 5 | 2Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 6 | 2Cext | analog | text: timing capacitor terminal |
| 7 | 2Rext/Cext | analog | text: timing resistor to VCC and capacitor terminal |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | 2A | input | text: negative-transition trigger input (fires on a falling edge while B is high) |
| 10 | 2B | input | text: positive-transition Schmitt-trigger input (fires on a rising edge while A is low); Schmitt input |
| 11 | 2CLR' | input | active: low; text: overriding clear: low ends the pulse, Q low; a rising edge with A low and B high also fires; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 12 | 2Q' | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | 1Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | 1Cext | analog | text: timing capacitor terminal |
| 15 | 1Rext/Cext | analog | text: timing resistor to VCC and capacitor terminal |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A | Q | low to high | - | 45 | 70 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, Cext 80 pF, Rext 2 kohm |
| B | Q | low to high | - | 35 | 55 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, Cext 80 pF, Rext 2 kohm |
| A | Q' | high to low | - | 50 | 80 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, Cext 80 pF, Rext 2 kohm |
| B | Q' | high to low | - | 40 | 65 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, Cext 80 pF, Rext 2 kohm |
| CLR' | Q | high to low | - | - | 27 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, Cext 80 pF, Rext 2 kohm |
| CLR' | Q' | low to high | - | - | 40 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm, Cext 80 pF, Rext 2 kohm |

- **Timing limits:** pulseWidth A or B minNs 50; pulseWidth CLR' minNs 20; setup CLR' inactive to trigger minNs 15; outputPulseWidth Q
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74221: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, TA 0-70 C. B input Schmitt: VT+ typ 1.55 V max 2 V, VT- min 0.8 typ 1.35 V; A input VT+ typ 1.4 max 2, VT- min 0.8 typ 1.4 V. Input edge rate: B min 1 V/s, A min 1 V/us. Rext 1.4-40 kohm, Cext 0-1000 uF. Duty cycle max 67% at Rext 2 kohm, 90% at max Rext. IIL A -1.6 mA, B and clear -3.2 mA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Typical clear-to-output delays are not printed (only max).; No minimum propagation delays printed.
- **Notes:** Pin-compatible with the 74123 except that it does not retrigger.

### 74S258 - Quadruple 2-line-to-1-line data selector multiplexer with tri-state outputs

- **Part:** 74S258
- **Description:** Quadruple 2-line-to-1-line data selector multiplexer with tri-state outputs
- **Formula:** nY = NOT ((SELECT' AND nA) OR (SELECT AND nB)), Z when G' high (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 40 (PDF page 46)

![74S258 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74S258.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74S258 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74S258.png) | ![74S258 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74S258.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book 2nd ed. 1976, SN54S257/SN54S258/SN74S257/SN74S258 (bulletin DL-S 7611734, Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-372, 7-375 (PDF pages 371, 374) (figures for SN74S258)
- **Formulas:**
  - `nY = NOT ((SELECT' AND nA) OR (SELECT AND nB)), Z when G' high` - Inverting: with G' low, Y is the complement of A (SELECT low) or of B (SELECT high); G' high puts all outputs in high impedance.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `1Y = NOT ((NOT SELECT AND 1A) OR (SELECT AND 1B))` (three-state) - drives when G' low, else Z
  - `2Y = NOT ((NOT SELECT AND 2A) OR (SELECT AND 2B))` (three-state) - drives when G' low, else Z
  - `3Y = NOT ((NOT SELECT AND 3A) OR (SELECT AND 3B))` (three-state) - drives when G' low, else Z
  - `4Y = NOT ((NOT SELECT AND 4A) OR (SELECT AND 4B))` (three-state) - drives when G' low, else Z

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |

| 1 | SELECT | input | text: low selects A inputs, high selects B inputs |
| 2 | 1A | input | - |
| 3 | 1B | input | - |
| 4 | 1Y | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: 15 |
| 5 | 2A | input | - |
| 6 | 2B | input | - |
| 7 | 2Y | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: 15 |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | 3Y | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: 15 |
| 10 | 3B | input | - |
| 11 | 3A | input | - |
| 12 | 4Y | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: 15 |
| 13 | 4B | input | - |
| 14 | 4A | input | - |
| 15 | G' | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; text: output control; controls: 4, 7, 9, 12 |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A or B | Y | low to high | - | 4 | 6 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| A or B | Y | high to low | - | 4 | 6 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| SELECT | Y | low to high | - | 8 | 12 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| SELECT | Y | high to low | - | 7.5 | 12 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| G' | Y | PZH | - | 13 | 19.5 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| G' | Y | PZL | - | 14 | 21 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 15 pF |
| G' | Y | PHZ | - | 5.5 | 8.5 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 5 pF |
| G' | Y | PLZ | - | 9 | 14 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, RL 280 ohm, CL 5 pF |

- **Output current:** low 20 mA, high -6.5 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74S258: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, IOH -6.5 mA, IOL 20 mA, TA 0-70 C. Off-state current +/-50 uA. S input IIL -4 mA, others -2 mA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** No minimum propagation delays printed.
- **Notes:** Function table rows: G'=H -> Z; G'=L, S=L, A=L -> H; A=H -> L; S=H, B=L -> H; B=H -> L. Output disable is faster than enable, by design.

### 74279 - Quadruple S-R latches

- **Part:** 74279
- **Description:** Quadruple S-R latches
- **Formula:** Q = latch state (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 41 (PDF page 47)

![74279 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74279.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74279 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74279.png) | ![74279 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74279.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book 2nd ed. 1976, SN54279/SN74279: pin assignment and function table on page 5-60; recommended conditions, electrical and switching characteristics on pages 6-60 and 6-61](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/05.pdf and https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/06.pdf) p. 5-60 (05.pdf PDF page 60), 6-60 and 6-61 (06.pdf PDF pages 60, 61) (figures for SN74279)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Q = latch state` - Each output is its latch's stored bit (two cross-coupled NAND gates). For latches 1 and 3, S' means both S' inputs: S'=H when both are high, S'=L when one or both are low.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on levels on S' and R' (no clock)
  - S'=H, R'=H: Q = Q0 (hold)
  - S'=L, R'=H: Q = H
  - S'=H, R'=L: Q = L
  - S'=L, R'=L: Q = H, pseudo-stable: it may not persist when S' and R' return to high
  - Latches 1 and 3: S' is low when either S1' or S2' is low
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `L1` (latch): `L1 = NOT (1S1' AND 1S2') OR (L1 AND 1R')` on while high: 1
  - stored `L2` (latch): `L2 = NOT (2S') OR (L2 AND 2R')` on while high: 1
  - stored `L3` (latch): `L3 = NOT (3S1' AND 3S2') OR (L3 AND 3R')` on while high: 1
  - stored `L4` (latch): `L4 = NOT (4S') OR (L4 AND 4R')` on while high: 1
  - `1Q = L1` (totem-pole)
  - `2Q = L2` (totem-pole)
  - `3Q = L3` (totem-pole)
  - `4Q = L4` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1R' | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 2 | 1S1' | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 3 | 1S2' | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 4 | 1Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 5 | 2R' | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 6 | 2S' | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 7 | 2Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | 3Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | 3R' | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 11 | 3S1' | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 12 | 3S2' | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 13 | 4Q | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | 4R' | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 15 | 4S' | input | active: low; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| S' | Q | low to high | - | 12 | 22 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| S' | Q | high to low | - | 9 | 15 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| R' | Q | high to low | - | 15 | 27 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74279: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, TA 0-70 C. Diode-clamped inputs, totem-pole outputs.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The datasheet lists tPHL from S' (9/15 ns) without saying which condition makes S' drive Q low; recorded as printed.; No minimum propagation delays printed.

### 74S280 - 9-bit odd/even parity generator/checker

- **Part:** 74S280
- **Description:** 9-bit odd/even parity generator/checker
- **Formula:** SUM EVEN = NOT (A XOR B XOR C XOR D XOR E XOR F XOR G XOR H XOR I); SUM ODD = A XOR B XOR C XOR D XOR E XOR F XOR G XOR H XOR I (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 41 (PDF page 47)

![74S280 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74S280.png)

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book 2nd ed. 1976, SN54LS280/SN54S280/SN74LS280/SN74S280 (bulletin DL-S 7611829, Dec 1972, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-406, 7-408 (PDF pages 405, 407) (figures for SN74S280)
- **Formulas:**

  - `SUM ODD = A XOR B XOR C XOR D XOR E XOR F XOR G XOR H XOR I` - High when an odd number (1,3,5,7,9) of inputs A-I are high.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `SUM EVEN = NOT (A XOR B XOR C XOR D XOR E XOR F XOR G XOR H XOR I)` (totem-pole)
  - `SUM ODD = A XOR B XOR C XOR D XOR E XOR F XOR G XOR H XOR I` (totem-pole)
