### 74298 - Quadruple 2-input multiplexer with storage

- **Part:** 74298
- **Description:** Quadruple 2-input multiplexer with storage
- **Formula:** QA..QD = stored bits (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 42 (PDF page 48)

![74298 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74298.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74298 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74298.png) | ![74298 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74298.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book 2nd ed. 1976, SN54298/SN74298 (bulletin DL-S 7611747, Mar 1974, rev. Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-432, 7-434 (PDF pages 431, 433) (figures for SN74298)
- **Formulas:**
  - `QA..QD = stored bits` - Outputs of the four flip-flops.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on falling edge of CLOCK
  - WORD SELECT low: QA..QD = A1,B1,C1,D1 at the falling clock edge
  - WORD SELECT high: QA..QD = A2,B2,C2,D2 at the falling clock edge
  - CLOCK high (no falling edge): hold
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FA` (flip-flop): `FA = (NOT WORD SELECT AND A1) OR (WORD SELECT AND A2)` on falling edge (H->L) of CLOCK
  - stored `FB` (flip-flop): `FB = (NOT WORD SELECT AND B1) OR (WORD SELECT AND B2)` on falling edge (H->L) of CLOCK
  - stored `FC` (flip-flop): `FC = (NOT WORD SELECT AND C1) OR (WORD SELECT AND C2)` on falling edge (H->L) of CLOCK
  - stored `FD` (flip-flop): `FD = (NOT WORD SELECT AND D1) OR (WORD SELECT AND D2)` on falling edge (H->L) of CLOCK
  - `QA = FA` (totem-pole)
  - `QB = FB` (totem-pole)
  - `QC = FC` (totem-pole)
  - `QD = FD` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | B2 | input | - |
| 2 | A2 | input | - |
| 3 | A1 | input | - |
| 4 | B1 | input | - |
| 5 | C2 | input | - |
| 6 | D2 | input | - |
| 7 | D1 | input | - |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | C1 | input | - |
| 10 | WORD SELECT | input | text: low: word 1 (A1,B1,C1,D1); high: word 2 (A2,B2,C2,D2) |
| 11 | CLOCK | clock | edge: falling |
| 12 | QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 15 | QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLOCK | QA..QD | low to high | - | 18 | 27 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | QA..QD | high to low | - | 21 | 32 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** pulseWidth CLOCK minNs 20; setup data to CLOCK falling minNs 15; setup WORD SELECT to CLOCK falling minNs 25; hold data to CLOCK falling minNs 5; hold WORD SELECT to CLOCK falling minNs 0
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74298: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, TA 0-70 C.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The switching table names no input for its single tPLH/tPHL pair; recorded as clock to output (the only path that drives the outputs).; No maximum clock frequency printed for the SN74298.; No minimum propagation delays printed.

### 74393 - Dual 4-bit binary ripple counter

- **Part:** 74393
- **Description:** Dual 4-bit binary ripple counter
- **Formula:** 1QA..1QD = counter 1 bits; 2QA..2QD = counter 2 bits (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 42 (PDF page 48)

![74393 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/74393.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![74393 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-74393.png) | ![74393 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-74393.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The TTL Data Book 2nd ed. 1976, SN54390/SN54393/SN74390/SN74393 (bulletin DL-S 7612099, Oct 1976)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1976_TI_The_TTL_Data_Book_2ed/07.pdf) p. 7-489, 7-492, 7-493 (PDF pages 488, 491, 492) (figures for SN74393)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1QA..1QD = counter 1 bits` - QA least significant.
  - `2QA..2QD = counter 2 bits` - QA least significant.
- **Stored state:** 8 bits, changes on falling edge of 1A / 2A (each counter separately)
  - nCLEAR high: nQA..nQD = L at once (positive logic note: high input to clear resets all four outputs low)
  - nCLEAR low, falling edge of nA: count = count + 1 (mod 16); ripple: QA toggles on the A edge, QB on QA falling, QC on QB falling, QD on QC falling
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `1FA` (counter): `1FA = NOT 1FA` on falling edge (H->L) of 1A; clear by 1CLEAR high
  - stored `1FB` (counter): `1FB = NOT 1FB` on falling edge (H->L) of 1FA; clear by 1CLEAR high
  - stored `1FC` (counter): `1FC = NOT 1FC` on falling edge (H->L) of 1FB; clear by 1CLEAR high
  - stored `1FD` (counter): `1FD = NOT 1FD` on falling edge (H->L) of 1FC; clear by 1CLEAR high
  - stored `2FA` (counter): `2FA = NOT 2FA` on falling edge (H->L) of 2A; clear by 2CLEAR high
  - stored `2FB` (counter): `2FB = NOT 2FB` on falling edge (H->L) of 2FA; clear by 2CLEAR high
  - stored `2FC` (counter): `2FC = NOT 2FC` on falling edge (H->L) of 2FB; clear by 2CLEAR high
  - stored `2FD` (counter): `2FD = NOT 2FD` on falling edge (H->L) of 2FC; clear by 2CLEAR high
  - `1QA = 1FA` (totem-pole)
  - `1QB = 1FB` (totem-pole)
  - `1QC = 1FC` (totem-pole)
  - `1QD = 1FD` (totem-pole)
  - `2QA = 2FA` (totem-pole)
  - `2QB = 2FB` (totem-pole)
  - `2QC = 2FC` (totem-pole)
  - `2QD = 2FD` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1A | clock | edge: falling; text: count input of counter 1 |
| 2 | 1CLEAR | input | active: high; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 3 | 1QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 4 | 1QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 5 | 1QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 6 | 1QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | 2QD | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 9 | 2QC | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | 2QB | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 11 | 2QA | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | 2CLEAR | input | active: high; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 13 | 2A | clock | edge: falling; text: count input of counter 2 |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A | QA | low to high | - | 12 | 20 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| A | QA | high to low | - | 13 | 20 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| A | QD | low to high | - | 40 | 60 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| A | QD | high to low | - | 40 | 60 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLEAR | any Q | high to low | - | 24 | 39 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** maxClockFrequency A minMHz 25 typMHz 35; pulseWidth A minNs 20; pulseWidth CLEAR minNs 20; setup CLEAR inactive to A falling minNs 25
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.8 mA
- **Electrical notes:** SN74393: VCC 4.75-5.25 V, TA 0-70 C. A input IIL -3.2 mA, clear -1 mA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** '393 delays from A to QB and QC are not printed (only to QA and QD).; No minimum propagation delays printed.
- **Notes:** Ripple-counter rule (QB clocked by QA etc.) is taken from the internal connection shown in the pin diagram and the 'ripple' description; the 7-489 page shows A feeding the first stage of each 4-bit counter.

### 75107 - Dual line receiver

- **Part:** 75107
- **Description:** Dual line receiver
- **Formula:** 1Y = NOT ((1A - 1B <= -25 mV) AND 1G AND S); 2Y = NOT ((2A - 2B <= -25 mV) AND 2G AND S) (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 43 (PDF page 49)

![75107 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/75107.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![75107 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-75107.png) | ![75107 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-75107.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The Interface Circuits Data Book for Design Engineers, 2nd ed. 1974, SN55107A/SN55108A/SN75107A/SN75108A (bulletin DL-S 7111224, Feb 1971)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1974_TI_The_Interface_Data_Book_for_Design_Engineers_Second_Edition.pdf) p. 4-6, 4-8, 4-9, 4-11 (PDF pages 70, 72, 73, 75) (figures for SN75107A)
- **Formulas:**
  - `1Y = NOT ((1A - 1B <= -25 mV) AND 1G AND S)` - Output low only when the differential input is negative (A below B by at least 25 mV) and both strobes are high; high otherwise.
  - `2Y = NOT ((2A - 2B <= -25 mV) AND 2G AND S)` - Same for channel 2.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - from analog levels: `1NEG` = 1 when 1A - 1B <= -25 mV, 0 when >= +25 mV; between, the output is not given (indeterminate)
  - from analog levels: `2NEG` = 1 when 2A - 2B <= -25 mV, 0 when >= +25 mV; between, the output is not given (indeterminate)
  - `1Y = NOT (1NEG AND 1G AND S)` (totem-pole)
  - `2Y = NOT (2NEG AND 2G AND S)` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1A | analog | text: non-inverting differential line input, channel 1 |
| 2 | 1B | analog | text: inverting differential line input, channel 1 |
| 3 | NC | nc | - |
| 4 | 1Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 5 | 1G | enable | active: high; whenInactive: high; text: strobe for channel 1: when 1G or S is low the output is high; controls: 4 |
| 6 | S | enable | active: high; whenInactive: high; text: strobe common to both channels; controls: 4, 9 |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | 2G | enable | active: high; whenInactive: high; text: strobe for channel 2; controls: 9 |
| 9 | 2Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 10 | NC | nc | - |
| 11 | 2B | analog | text: inverting differential line input, channel 2 |
| 12 | 2A | analog | text: non-inverting differential line input, channel 2 |
| 13 | VCC- | power | voltage: -5 V |
| 14 | VCC+ | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A,B (differential) | Y | low to high | - | 17 | 25 | VCC+ 5 V, VCC- -5 V, TA 25 C, RL 390 ohm, CL 50 pF |
| A,B (differential) | Y | high to low | - | 17 | 25 | VCC+ 5 V, VCC- -5 V, TA 25 C, RL 390 ohm, CL 50 pF |
| G or S | Y | low to high | - | 10 | 15 | VCC+ 5 V, VCC- -5 V, TA 25 C, RL 390 ohm, CL 50 pF |
| G or S | Y | high to low | - | 8 | 15 | VCC+ 5 V, VCC- -5 V, TA 25 C, RL 390 ohm, CL 50 pF |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.4 mA
- **Electrical notes:** VCC+ 4.75-5.25 V, VCC- -4.75 to -5.25 V, TA 0-70 C. Receiver input thresholds: VIDH 25 mV (output high when A-B >= +25 mV), VIDL -25 mV; between them the output is indeterminate when both strobes are high. Differential input range -5 to +5 V, common-mode range -3 to +3 V. Strobe inputs: VIH 2 V, VIL 0.8 V. VOH min 2.4 V at Iload -400 uA.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** No minimum propagation delays printed.; Datasheet read is the improved 'A' version (SN75107A/SN75108A), described as a direct replacement for the original; the original non-A datasheet was not read.
- **Notes:** Truth table: VID >= 25 mV -> H; -25 < VID < 25 mV -> H unless both G and S high (then indeterminate); VID <= -25 mV -> L only with G and S both high. When only one channel is used, ground the other channel's inputs.

### 75109 - Dual line driver (75109-75110)

- **Part:** 75109
- **Description:** Dual line driver (75109-75110)
- **Formula:** Y on (low) = C AND D AND NOT (A AND B); otherwise off (high); Z on (low) = C AND D AND A AND B; otherwise off (high) (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 43 (PDF page 49)

![75109 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/75109.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![75109 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-75109.png) | ![75109 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-75109.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The Interface Circuits Data Book 2nd ed. 1974, SN55109/SN55110/SN75109/SN75110 (bulletin DL-S 7111224)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1974_TI_The_Interface_Data_Book_for_Design_Engineers_Second_Edition.pdf) p. 4-6, 4-16, 4-17 (PDF pages 70, 80, 81) (figures for SN75109 (SN75110 figures noted))
- **Formulas:**
  - `Y on (low) = C AND D AND NOT (A AND B); otherwise off (high)` - Truth table: with both inhibitors high, Y is on when A or B is low; when A and B are both high, Y is off and Z is on.
  - `Z on (low) = C AND D AND A AND B; otherwise off (high)` - Complementary current output of the same channel. Either inhibitor low turns both Y and Z off.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `1Y = 1A AND 1B` (open-collector) - drives when 1C high and D high, else 1
  - `1Z = NOT (1A AND 1B)` (open-collector) - drives when 1C high and D high, else 1
  - `2Y = 2A AND 2B` (open-collector) - drives when 2C high and D high, else 1
  - `2Z = NOT (2A AND 2B)` (open-collector) - drives when 2C high and D high, else 1

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1A | input | - |
| 2 | 1B | input | - |
| 3 | 1C | enable | active: high; whenInactive: float; text: inhibitor for channel 1: low turns both outputs of channel 1 off; controls: 13, 12 |
| 4 | 2C | enable | active: high; whenInactive: float; text: inhibitor for channel 2; controls: 8, 9 |
| 5 | 2A | input | - |
| 6 | 2B | input | - |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | 2Y | output | outputType: open-collector; text: current-switch output (sinks the on-state current when on, off = high impedance); low = on, high = off |
| 9 | 2Z | output | outputType: open-collector; text: current-switch output (sinks the on-state current when on, off = high impedance); low = on, high = off |
| 10 | D | enable | active: high; whenInactive: float; text: inhibitor common to both channels; controls: 13, 12, 8, 9 |
| 11 | VCC- | power | voltage: -5 V |
| 12 | 1Z | output | outputType: open-collector; text: current-switch output (sinks the on-state current when on, off = high impedance); low = on, high = off |
| 13 | 1Y | output | outputType: open-collector; text: current-switch output (sinks the on-state current when on, off = high impedance); low = on, high = off |
| 14 | VCC+ | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A or B | Y or Z | low to high | - | 9 | 15 | VCC+ 5 V, VCC- -5 V, TA 25 C, RL 50 ohm, CL 40 pF |
| A or B | Y or Z | high to low | - | 9 | 15 | VCC+ 5 V, VCC- -5 V, TA 25 C, RL 50 ohm, CL 40 pF |
| C or D | Y or Z | low to high | - | 16 | 25 | VCC+ 5 V, VCC- -5 V, TA 25 C, RL 50 ohm, CL 40 pF |
| C or D | Y or Z | high to low | - | 13 | 25 | VCC+ 5 V, VCC- -5 V, TA 25 C, RL 50 ohm, CL 40 pF |

- **Output current:** low - mA, high - mA; output may be pulled up to 10 V
- **Electrical notes:** VCC+ 4.75-5.25 V, VCC- -4.75 to -5.25 V, TA 0-70 C. Current-mode outputs: on-state output current SN75109 min 3.5 mA, max 7 mA (SN75110: 6.5 to 15 mA); off-state current max 100 uA. Common-mode output voltage range 0 to +10 V and 0 to -3 V (recommended). Logic and inhibitor inputs VIH 2 V, VIL 0.8 V. When one channel is unused, inhibit it and/or ground its outputs.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** IOL/IOH are not meaningful for these current-switch outputs; the datasheet gives on-state current instead (recorded in notes), so both current fields are null.; No minimum propagation delays printed.
- **Notes:** ND symbol covers 75109 and 75110; they differ only in on-state current (75109 about 6 mA, 75110 about 12 mA typical per page 4-6).

### 75150 - 2-input line driver

- **Formula:** Y = NOT(A AND S)

- **Timing limits:** transitionTime Y
- **Output current:** low - mA, high - mA; output may be pulled up to 15 V
- **Electrical notes:** VCC+ 10.8-13.2 V, VCC- -10.8 to -13.2 V, TA 0-70 C. Applied output voltage max +/-15 V (recommended); withstands short circuit to -25..+25 V. Output levels at RL 3-7 kohm: VOH min 5 V typ 8 V; VOL typ -8 V max -5 V. Inputs TTL/DTL: VIH 2 V, VIL 0.8 V, input voltage 0-5.5 V. Short-circuit current at VO 0 V: 15 mA (VI 3 V), -15 mA (VI 0 V) typical. Slew rate can be set with an external capacitor at the output.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Recommended IOL/IOH are not given (RS-232 line driver; output levels are given into 3-7 kohm instead).; Maximum propagation delays are not printed (typical only).; The datasheet's delay table does not say whether the A or the S input was switched.
- **Notes:** The ND symbol draws one driver (Y = NOT(A AND S)); the package holds two drivers sharing S.

### 75154 - Line driver

- **Part:** 75154
- **Description:** Line driver
- **Formula:** Y = NOT A
- **Manual page:** 44 (PDF page 50)

![75154 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/75154.png)

| ND 1976 | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![line-driver1 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/line-driver1.png) | ![line-driver1 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/line-driver1.png) |

- **Output:** line
- **What it looks like:** A large open triangle pointing right with one input.
- **How to tell it apart:** Big triangle, one input. The manual heads it 'Line driver', but the 75154 is a quadruple line RECEIVER in every datasheet found (TI 1974 and 1995, National DS75154), and sheet 2A50 uses it as a receiver; the manual's label is kept here as printed and taken to be a slip (see catalog/chips/75154.json).

- **Datasheet:** [TI The Interface Circuits Data Book 2nd ed. 1974, SN75154 quadruple line receiver (bulletin DL-S 7011389, Nov 1970)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1974_TI_The_Interface_Data_Book_for_Design_Engineers_Second_Edition.pdf) p. 4-79 to 4-81 (PDF pages 143-145); the schematic with R1 is on 4-81 (figures for SN75154); [TI SN75154 Quadruple Line Receiver, revision B, May 1995](https://www.ti.com/lit/gpn/sn75154) p. 1 (pin diagram footnote 'For function of R1, see schematic') (figures for SN75154); [National Semiconductor DS75154 quad line receiver](https://pccomponents.com/datasheets/NSC75154.PDF) p. 1 (description; read by text search of the PDF, not page by page) (figures for DS75154)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Y = NOT A (with hysteresis: low when A > VT+, high when A < VT-, else holds)` - Datasheet logic Y = A'. Inverting RS-232-C receiver with active pull-up output. In fail-safe mode (threshold control open) an open or zero input gives a high output.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on input crossing a threshold (hysteresis)
  - Normal operation (T pin to VCC1): output goes low when A rises above VT+ (0.8 min, 2.2 typ, 3 max V), high when A falls below VT- (-3 min, -1.1 typ, 0 max V); between, output keeps its previous level; input at 0 V keeps the previous level
  - Fail-safe operation (T pin open): VT+ 0.8/2.2/3 V, VT- 0.8/1.4/3 V (both above zero); open or 0 V input gives output high
  - Hysteresis VT+ - VT-: normal 0.8/3.3/6 V, fail-safe 0/0.8/2.2 V
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - from analog levels: `1HI` = 1 once 1A rises above VT+ (normal: 0.8/2.2/3 V), 0 once it falls below VT- (normal: -3/-1.1/0 V), keeps its value between (hysteresis); fail-safe mode (T open): VT+ 0.8/2.2/3 V, VT- 0.8/1.4/3 V, open input gives 0
  - from analog levels: `2HI` = 1 once 2A rises above VT+ (normal: 0.8/2.2/3 V), 0 once it falls below VT- (normal: -3/-1.1/0 V), keeps its value between (hysteresis); fail-safe mode (T open): VT+ 0.8/2.2/3 V, VT- 0.8/1.4/3 V, open input gives 0
  - from analog levels: `3HI` = 1 once 3A rises above VT+ (normal: 0.8/2.2/3 V), 0 once it falls below VT- (normal: -3/-1.1/0 V), keeps its value between (hysteresis); fail-safe mode (T open): VT+ 0.8/2.2/3 V, VT- 0.8/1.4/3 V, open input gives 0
  - from analog levels: `4HI` = 1 once 4A rises above VT+ (normal: 0.8/2.2/3 V), 0 once it falls below VT- (normal: -3/-1.1/0 V), keeps its value between (hysteresis); fail-safe mode (T open): VT+ 0.8/2.2/3 V, VT- 0.8/1.4/3 V, open input gives 0
  - `1Y = NOT 1HI` (totem-pole)
  - `2Y = NOT 2HI` (totem-pole)
  - `3Y = NOT 3HI` (totem-pole)
  - `4Y = NOT 4HI` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 3T | analog | text: threshold control |
| 2 | 2T | analog | text: threshold control |
| 3 | 1T | analog | text: threshold control: to VCC1 for normal (hysteresis) operation, open for fail-safe |
| 4 | 1A | analog | text: RS-232-C line input |
| 5 | 2A | analog | text: RS-232-C line input |
| 6 | 3A | analog | text: RS-232-C line input |
| 7 | 4A | analog | text: RS-232-C line input |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | R1 | analog | text: R1: a 5 kohm resistor inside the chip from this pin to VCC1 (pin 15), in the block common to all four receivers and connected to nothing else - a spare pull-up to +5 V (TI 1974 schematic, page 4-81) |
| 10 | 4Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 11 | 3Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 12 | 2Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | 1Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | 4T | analog | text: threshold control |
| 15 | VCC1 | power | voltage: +5 V; text: normal supply |
| 16 | VCC2 | power | voltage: +12 V; text: alternate supply (10.8-13.2 V) when no 5 V supply is used |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A | Y | low to high | - | 22 | - | VCC1 5 V, TA 25 C, N 10, CL 50 pF, RL 390 ohm |
| A | Y | high to low | - | 20 | - | VCC1 5 V, TA 25 C, N 10, CL 50 pF, RL 390 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** transitionTime Y
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.4 mA
- **Electrical notes:** VCC1 4.5-5.5 V (or VCC2 10.8-13.2 V), TA 0-70 C. Input voltage range +/-15 V recommended (+/-25 V absolute). Input resistance 3-7 kohm. Open-circuit input voltage 0-2 V. VOH min 2.4 V at IOH -400 uA, VOL max 0.4 V at IOL 16 mA (these are test conditions; the recommended conditions give fan-out N = 10). VIH 3 V, VIL -3 V.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Only typical delays are printed; no min or max.; IOL/IOH are taken from the VOL/VOH test conditions; the recommended conditions give fan-out N = 10 instead.
- **Notes:** ND-13.003.01 page 44 labels this part 'Line driver'. Every datasheet found (TI 1974, TI 1995, National DS75154) calls it a quadruple line receiver and no 75154 sold as a driver was found, so the manual's label is taken as a slip on that one entry (the 75150 above it is a driver). NORD-10 sheet 2A50 uses two 75154 as receivers for the modem's incoming signals. Y = NOT A agrees with the datasheet logic.

Apr 1977)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1981_TI_The_Interface_Circuits_Data_Book_for_Design_Engineers_2ed.pdf) p. 7-13 to 7-15 (PDF pages 517-519) (figures for SN75322)
- **Formulas:**

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1XC | analog | text: connection to the collector of the external p-n-p transistor for driver 1 |
| 2 | 1XB | analog | text: connection to the base of the external p-n-p transistor for driver 1 |
| 3 | 1A | input | - |
| 4 | E | input | text: strobe common to both drivers |
| 5 | 2A | input | - |
| 6 | NC | nc | - |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | 2XB | analog | text: base connection of the external p-n-p for driver 2 |
| 9 | 2XC | analog | text: collector connection of the external p-n-p for driver 2 |
| 10 | 2Y | output | outputType: totem-pole; text: MOS-level output |
| 11 | VCC2 | power | voltage: +12 V nominal (4.75-15 V); text: MOS supply |
| 12 | 1Y | output | outputType: totem-pole; text: MOS-level output, pulled up through the external p-n-p transistor |
| 13 | NC | nc | - |
| 14 | VCC1 | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A or E | Y | low to high | 12 | 27 | 38 | VCC1 5 V, VCC2 12 V, TA 25 C, CL 300 pF, external TIS149 p-n-p |
| A or E | Y | high to low | 14 | 31 | 44 | VCC1 5 V, VCC2 12 V, TA 25 C, CL 300 pF, external TIS149 p-n-p |

- **Timing limits:** delayTime Y; transitionTime Y
- **Output current:** low - mA, high - mA; output may be pulled up to 15 V
- **Electrical notes:** VCC1 4.75-5.25 V, VCC2 4.75-15 V (nom 12), TA 0-70 C, load capacitance min 200 pF (recommended). VOH min VCC2-1.1 V at -10 mA, VCC2-0.5 at -400 uA, VCC2-0.4 at -200 uA; VOL max 0.5 V at IOL 10 mA (VCC2 11.4 V). Inputs VIH 2 V, VIL 0.8 V; IIL A -1.6 mA, E -3.2 mA. Output goes to high impedance if the 5 V supply is lost (forced low with an external pull-down). outputMaxVoltageV is the VCC2 maximum.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Recommended IOL/IOH are not given; only VOH/VOL test points (in notes), so the current fields are null.

### 75450 - Dual peripheral driver

- **Part:** 75450
- **Description:** Dual peripheral driver
- **Formula:** Y = NOT (A AND G); C = A AND G (with Y wired to B and E to ground) (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 45 (PDF page 51)

![75450 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/75450.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![75450 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-75450.png) | ![75450 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-75450.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [TI The Interface Circuits Data Book 2nd ed. 1974, Series 55450B/75450B dual peripheral drivers (bulletin DL-S 7311798, Sep 1973, tentative data sheet)](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1974_TI_The_Interface_Data_Book_for_Design_Engineers_Second_Edition.pdf) p. 6-42, 6-43, 6-46, 6-47 (PDF pages 272, 273, 276, 277) (figures for SN75450B (pages titled 'TYPE SN75450'))
- **Formulas:**
  - `Y = NOT (A AND G)` - TTL NAND gate (datasheet: Y = (AG)', gate only).
  - `C = A AND G (with Y wired to B and E to ground)` - Datasheet: C = AG for gate and transistor together; C is off (high, pulled up externally) when A and G are high, on (low) otherwise.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `1Y = NOT (1A AND G)` (totem-pole)
  - `1C = 1A AND G` (open-collector-high-voltage)
  - `2Y = NOT (2A AND G)` (totem-pole)
  - `2C = 2A AND G` (open-collector-high-voltage)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | G | input | text: input common to both NAND gates |
| 2 | 1A | input | - |
| 3 | 1Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 4 | 1B | analog | text: base of output transistor 1 (normally wired to 1Y) |
| 5 | 1C | output | outputType: open-collector-high-voltage; text: collector of output transistor 1 |
| 6 | 1E | analog | text: emitter of output transistor 1 (normally grounded) |
| 7 | GND | ground | - |
| 8 | SUB | ground | text: substrate: must always be at the most negative voltage of the device |
| 9 | 2E | analog | text: emitter of output transistor 2 |
| 10 | 2C | output | outputType: open-collector-high-voltage; text: collector of output transistor 2 |
| 11 | 2B | analog | text: base of output transistor 2 |
| 12 | 2Y | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | 2A | input | - |
| 14 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A or G | Y | low to high | - | 12 | 22 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| A or G | Y | high to low | - | 8 | 15 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| A or G | C (gate and transistor) | low to high | - | 20 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, IC ~200 mA, CL 15 pF, RL 50 ohm |
| A or G | C (gate and transistor) | high to low | - | 20 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, IC ~200 mA, CL 15 pF, RL 50 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** transistorSwitching C
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -0.4 mA; output may be pulled up to 30 V
- **Electrical notes:** VCC 4.75-5.25 V, TA 0-70 C. Gate outputs Y: VOL 0.4 V at 16 mA, VOH 2.4 V at -400 uA (test conditions). Transistors: abs max collector current 300 mA, collector-emitter 30 V (RBE <= 500 ohm), collector-base 35 V, emitter-base 5 V; VCE(sat) 0.25/0.4 V at 100 mA, 0.5/0.7 V at 300 mA. The fields here give the gate output figures; outputMaxVoltageV 30 V is the transistor collector-emitter rating.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Recommended operating conditions give no output currents; IOL/IOH are the gate VOL/VOH test conditions. The 300 mA transistor current is an absolute maximum, not a recommended figure.; Datasheet read is the 'B' version (replaces the original 75450).; No minimum delays printed.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1976 TTL Databook, DM70/DM8095,L95,96,L96,97,L97,98,L98 TRI-STATE hex buffers](https://bitsavers.mirrorservice.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1976_National_TTL_Databook.pdf) p. 3-7, 3-8 (PDF pages 352, 353) (figures for DM8095)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Y = A when G1' = L AND G2' = L; Z when G1' = H OR G2' = H` - Non-inverting buffers; all six share the two control lines. Truth table: G1' H or G2' H -> Hi-Z; both low -> Y = A.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `Y1 = A1` (three-state) - drives when G1' low and G2' low, else Z
  - `Y2 = A2` (three-state) - drives when G1' low and G2' low, else Z
  - `Y3 = A3` (three-state) - drives when G1' low and G2' low, else Z
  - `Y4 = A4` (three-state) - drives when G1' low and G2' low, else Z
  - `Y5 = A5` (three-state) - drives when G1' low and G2' low, else Z
  - `Y6 = A6` (three-state) - drives when G1' low and G2' low, else Z

### 8123 - Tri-state quad 2-input multiplexer

- **Part:** 8123
- **Description:** Tri-state quad 2-input multiplexer
- **Formula:** Yn = (S' AND An) OR (S AND Bn) when G'; Z when G high (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 48 (PDF page 54)

![8123 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/8123.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![8123 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-8123.png) | ![8123 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-8123.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1976 TTL Databook, DM71/DM81L22,23,L23 Quad 2-Input Data Selectors/Multiplexers](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1976_National_TTL_Databook.pdf) p. pdf 358-360 (printed 3-13..3-15) (figures for DM8123)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Yn = (S' AND An) OR (S AND Bn) when G'; Z when G high` - With G (strobe/enable) low, each Y follows its A input when S is low and its B input when S is high. With G high all four outputs float.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `Y1 = (NOT S AND A1) OR (S AND B1)` (three-state) - drives when G low, else Z
  - `Y2 = (NOT S AND A2) OR (S AND B2)` (three-state) - drives when G low, else Z
  - `Y3 = (NOT S AND A3) OR (S AND B3)` (three-state) - drives when G low, else Z
  - `Y4 = (NOT S AND A4) OR (S AND B4)` (three-state) - drives when G low, else Z

### 8160 - 6-bit comparator

- **Part:** 8160
- **Description:** 6-bit comparator
- **Formula:** Y = STROBE OR ((A1 XNOR B1) AND (A2 XNOR B2) AND (A3 XNOR B3) AND (A4 XNOR B4) AND (A5 XNOR B5) AND (A6 XNOR B6)) (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 49 (PDF page 55)

![8160 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/8160.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![8160 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-8160.png) | ![8160 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-8160.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1976 TTL Databook, DM71/DM8130,60 Magnitude Comparators](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1976_National_TTL_Databook.pdf) p. pdf 362-363 (printed 3-17, 3-18) (figures for DM8160)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Y = STROBE OR ((A1 XNOR B1) AND (A2 XNOR B2) AND (A3 XNOR B3) AND (A4 XNOR B4) AND (A5 XNOR B5) AND (A6 XNOR B6))` - Y is high (output transistor off) when the two 6-bit words are equal, or when STROBE is high. Y is low when STROBE is low and the words differ. Open collector, so several can be wired together.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `Y = STROBE OR (NOT (A1 XOR B1) AND NOT (A2 XOR B2) AND NOT (A3 XOR B3) AND NOT (A4 XOR B4) AND NOT (A5 XOR B5) AND NOT (A6 XOR B6))` (open-collector)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | A1 | input | - |
| 2 | B1 | input | - |
| 3 | A2 | input | - |
| 4 | B2 | input | - |
| 5 | A3 | input | - |
| 6 | B3 | input | - |
| 7 | STROBE | input | - |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | Y | output | outputType: open-collector |
| 10 | B4 | input | - |
| 11 | A4 | input | - |
| 12 | B5 | input | - |
| 13 | A5 | input | - |
| 14 | B6 | input | - |
| 15 | A6 | input | - |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A/B | Y | low to high | - | 15 | 25 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| A/B | Y | high to low | - | 27 | 40 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| STROBE | Y | low to high | - | 9 | 18 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| STROBE | Y | high to low | - | 20 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 15 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high - mA; output may be pulled up to 5.5 V
- **Electrical notes:** Open collector: IOH 100 uA max leakage at VOH 5.5 V.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** No minimum delays printed.; The pin-7 label in the diagram carries a bar, but the description and truth table say a HIGH strobe forces Y high; the formula follows the truth table.
- **Notes:** National calls it a "magnitude comparator" but it only tests for equality.

### 8214 - Tri-state dual 4:1 multiplexer

- **Part:** 8214
- **Description:** Tri-state dual 4:1 multiplexer
- **Formula:** Y1 = (B'A' AND 1C0) OR (B'A AND 1C1) OR (BA' AND 1C2) OR (BA AND 1C3) when G1'; Z when G1 high; Y2 = (B'A' AND 2C0) OR (B'A AND 2C1) OR (BA' AND 2C2) OR (BA AND 2C3) when G2'; Z when G2 high (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 49 (PDF page 55)

![8214 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/8214.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![8214 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-8214.png) | ![8214 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-8214.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1976 TTL Databook, DM72/DM8214,19 TRI-STATE Data Selectors/Multiplexers](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1976_National_TTL_Databook.pdf) p. pdf 373-376 (printed 3-28..3-31) (figures for DM8214)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Y1 = (B'A' AND 1C0) OR (B'A AND 1C1) OR (BA' AND 1C2) OR (BA AND 1C3) when G1'; Z when G1 high` - Y1 gives the 1C input picked by B,A (true data) while G1 is low; floats while G1 is high.
  - `Y2 = (B'A' AND 2C0) OR (B'A AND 2C1) OR (BA' AND 2C2) OR (BA AND 2C3) when G2'; Z when G2 high` - Same for section 2 with G2.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `Y1 = (B'A' AND 1C0) OR (B'A AND 1C1) OR (BA' AND 1C2) OR (BA AND 1C3)` (three-state) - drives when G1 low, else Z
  - `Y2 = (B'A' AND 2C0) OR (B'A AND 2C1) OR (BA' AND 2C2) OR (BA AND 2C3)` (three-state) - drives when G2 low, else Z

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |

### 8551 - Tri-state quad D flip flop (see 74173)

- **Part:** 8551
- **Description:** Tri-state quad D flip flop (see 74173)
- **Formula:** Qn = stored bit n when M' AND N'; Z when M OR N (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 50 (PDF page 56)

![8551 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/8551.png)

- **Drawing:** the manual prints no drawing for this part, only a reference to another part.

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1976 TTL Databook, DM75/DM8551,L51 TRI-STATE 4-Bit D Type Registers](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1976_National_TTL_Databook.pdf) p. pdf 407-408 (printed 3-62, 3-63) (figures for DM8551)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Qn = stored bit n when M' AND N'; Z when M OR N` - Each Q shows its flip-flop while M and N are both low; if either is high the outputs float. The flip-flops keep working while the outputs float.
- **Stored state:** 4 bits, changes on rising edge of CLOCK
  - CLEAR high: all four bits = 0 at once
  - CLEAR low, G1 and G2 both low: bit n = Dn at the rising CLOCK edge
  - CLEAR low, G1 or G2 high: bits hold at the clock edge
  - CLOCK low: bits hold
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `FF1` (flip-flop): `FF1 = ((G1 OR G2) AND FF1) OR (G1' AND G2' AND D1)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR high
  - stored `FF2` (flip-flop): `FF2 = ((G1 OR G2) AND FF2) OR (G1' AND G2' AND D2)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR high
  - stored `FF3` (flip-flop): `FF3 = ((G1 OR G2) AND FF3) OR (G1' AND G2' AND D3)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR high
  - stored `FF4` (flip-flop): `FF4 = ((G1 OR G2) AND FF4) OR (G1' AND G2' AND D4)` on rising edge (L->H) of CLOCK; clear by CLEAR high
  - `Q1 = FF1` (three-state) - drives when M low and N low, else Z
  - `Q2 = FF2` (three-state) - drives when M low and N low, else Z
  - `Q3 = FF3` (three-state) - drives when M low and N low, else Z
  - `Q4 = FF4` (three-state) - drives when M low and N low, else Z

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | M | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; controls: 3, 4, 5, 6 |
| 2 | N | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; controls: 3, 4, 5, 6 |
| 3 | Q1 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: M,N |
| 4 | Q2 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: M,N |
| 5 | Q3 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: M,N |
| 6 | Q4 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: M,N |
| 7 | CLOCK | clock | edge: rising |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | G1 | input | - |
| 10 | G2 | input | - |
| 11 | D4 | input | - |
| 12 | D3 | input | - |
| 13 | D2 | input | - |
| 14 | D1 | input | - |
| 15 | CLEAR | input | active: high; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CLEAR | Q | high to low | - | 18 | 27 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 50 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | Q | low to high | - | 16 | 25 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 50 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| CLOCK | Q | high to low | - | 20 | 28 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 50 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| M/N | Q | PZH | 7 | 16 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 50 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| M/N | Q | PZL | 7 | 21 | 30 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 50 pF, RL 400 ohm |
| M/N | Q | PHZ | 3 | 5 | 14 | VCC 5 V, TA 25 C, CL 5 pF, RL 400 ohm |

- **Timing limits:** pulseWidth CLOCK minNs 20; pulseWidth CLEAR minNs 20; setup G1/G2 to CLOCK minNs 17; setup D to CLOCK minNs 10; setup CLEAR inactive to CLOCK minNs 10; hold G1/G2 to CLOCK minNs 2; hold D to CLOCK minNs 10; maxClockFrequency CLOCK minMHz 25 typMHz 30
- **Output current:** low 16 mA, high -5.2 mA
- **Electrical notes:** DM85 column.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Enable/disable times: the text layer gives three numbers for the 51 column; read as min/typ/max, not checked against the page image.; Setup/hold/pulse-width figures read as minimums from the DM75/85 51 column.
- **Notes:** Same function as 74173. Data enable (G1, G2) and output control (M, N) are separate.

### 8553 - Tri-state 8 bit latch

- **Part:** 8553
- **Description:** Tri-state 8 bit latch
- **Formula:** IOn = latch n when ENABLE' AND READ1' AND READ2' AND WRITE1 AND WRITE2; otherwise Z (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 50 (PDF page 56)

![8553 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/8553.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![8553 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-8553.png) | ![8553 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-8553.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1976 TTL Databook, DM75/DM8553 TRI-STATE 8-Bit Latches](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1976_National_TTL_Databook.pdf) p. pdf 415-416 (printed 3-70, 3-71) (figures for DM8553)
- **Formulas:**
  - `IOn = latch n when ENABLE' AND READ1' AND READ2' AND WRITE1 AND WRITE2; otherwise Z` - The I/O pins drive the stored byte only when ENABLE and both READ inputs are low and both WRITE inputs are high (read). Otherwise they float and act as inputs.
- **Stored state:** 8 bits, changes on level (transparent latch while writing)
  - CLEAR H, ENABLE L, READ L, WRITE H: enter L into all latches, bus shows L
  - CLEAR H, ENABLE L, READ L, WRITE L: enter L, bus Hi-Z
  - CLEAR L, ENABLE L, READ H, WRITE H: do nothing, bus Hi-Z
  - CLEAR L, ENABLE H: do nothing, bus Hi-Z
  - CLEAR L, ENABLE L, WRITE L: write, latches take the bus levels
  - CLEAR L, ENABLE L, READ L, WRITE H: read, bus shows the latches
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - stored `L1` (latch): `L1 = NOT CLEAR AND IO1` on while high: CLEAR OR (NOT ENABLE AND NOT WRITE1 AND NOT WRITE2)
  - stored `L2` (latch): `L2 = NOT CLEAR AND IO2` on while high: CLEAR OR (NOT ENABLE AND NOT WRITE1 AND NOT WRITE2)
  - stored `L3` (latch): `L3 = NOT CLEAR AND IO3` on while high: CLEAR OR (NOT ENABLE AND NOT WRITE1 AND NOT WRITE2)
  - stored `L4` (latch): `L4 = NOT CLEAR AND IO4` on while high: CLEAR OR (NOT ENABLE AND NOT WRITE1 AND NOT WRITE2)
  - stored `L5` (latch): `L5 = NOT CLEAR AND IO5` on while high: CLEAR OR (NOT ENABLE AND NOT WRITE1 AND NOT WRITE2)
  - stored `L6` (latch): `L6 = NOT CLEAR AND IO6` on while high: CLEAR OR (NOT ENABLE AND NOT WRITE1 AND NOT WRITE2)
  - stored `L7` (latch): `L7 = NOT CLEAR AND IO7` on while high: CLEAR OR (NOT ENABLE AND NOT WRITE1 AND NOT WRITE2)
  - stored `L8` (latch): `L8 = NOT CLEAR AND IO8` on while high: CLEAR OR (NOT ENABLE AND NOT WRITE1 AND NOT WRITE2)
  - `IO1 = L1` (three-state) - drives when ENABLE low and READ1 low and READ2 low and WRITE1 high and WRITE2 high, else Z
  - `IO2 = L2` (three-state) - drives when ENABLE low and READ1 low and READ2 low and WRITE1 high and WRITE2 high, else Z
  - `IO3 = L3` (three-state) - drives when ENABLE low and READ1 low and READ2 low and WRITE1 high and WRITE2 high, else Z
  - `IO4 = L4` (three-state) - drives when ENABLE low and READ1 low and READ2 low and WRITE1 high and WRITE2 high, else Z
  - `IO5 = L5` (three-state) - drives when ENABLE low and READ1 low and READ2 low and WRITE1 high and WRITE2 high, else Z
  - `IO6 = L6` (three-state) - drives when ENABLE low and READ1 low and READ2 low and WRITE1 high and WRITE2 high, else Z
  - `IO7 = L7` (three-state) - drives when ENABLE low and READ1 low and READ2 low and WRITE1 high and WRITE2 high, else Z
  - `IO8 = L8` (three-state) - drives when ENABLE low and READ1 low and READ2 low and WRITE1 high and WRITE2 high, else Z

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | READ1 | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; controls: 4, 5, 6, 7, 9, 10, 11, 12 |
| 2 | ENABLE | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; controls: 4, 5, 6, 7, 9, 10, 11, 12 |
| 3 | CLEAR | input | active: high; acts at once (not on a clock edge) |
| 4 | IO1 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ENABLE,READ1,READ2; text: bidirectional: data input when floating, latch output when read |
| 5 | IO2 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ENABLE,READ1,READ2; text: bidirectional: data input when floating, latch output when read |
| 6 | IO3 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ENABLE,READ1,READ2; text: bidirectional: data input when floating, latch output when read |
| 7 | IO4 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ENABLE,READ1,READ2; text: bidirectional: data input when floating, latch output when read |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |

- **Electrical notes:** DM85 column.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The reference signal for data setup/hold (taken as WRITE) is not named in the text read; the timing waveform was not read.; In the truth table READ and WRITE mean "both READ inputs" / "both WRITE inputs"; behaviour with the two READ (or two WRITE) pins at different levels is not stated.; No minimum delays printed.

### 8599 - Tri-state 64-bit random access read/write memory

- **Part:** 8599
- **Description:** Tri-state 64-bit random access read/write memory
- **Formula:** Sn = NOT mem[A3..A0][n] when ME' AND WE; Z when ME high (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 51 (PDF page 57)

![8599 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/8599.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![8599 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-8599.png) | ![8599 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-8599.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1976 TTL Databook, DM75/DM8599 TRI-STATE 64-Bit Random Access Memories](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1976_National_TTL_Databook.pdf) p. pdf 472-475 (printed 3-127..3-130) (figures for DM8599)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Sn = NOT mem[A3..A0][n] when ME' AND WE; Z when ME high` - With ME low and WE high, S1..S4 show the COMPLEMENT of the 4-bit word stored at address A3..A0. With ME high the outputs float.
- **Stored state:** 64 bits, changes on level: ME low and WE low
  - ME L, WE L: D1..D4 written into word A3..A0
  - ME L, WE H: read, outputs show the complement of the stored word
  - ME H: hold, outputs Hi-Z
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - memory `M`: 16 words x 4 bits, address A0, A1, A2, A3
  - `S1 = NOT (WE AND M1)` (three-state) - drives when ME low, else Z
  - `S2 = NOT (WE AND M2)` (three-state) - drives when ME low, else Z
  - `S3 = NOT (WE AND M3)` (three-state) - drives when ME low, else Z
  - `S4 = NOT (WE AND M4)` (three-state) - drives when ME low, else Z

| 7 | S2 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ME |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |

| 15 | A1 | input | - |

- **Formulas:**
  - `-` - Differential line receiver: the output is high when IN+ is above IN- by more than the threshold (0 V typ, +-0.5 V max with no common mode, +-1 V over +-15 V common mode), low when below. - **Not settled by the datasheet:** STROBE polarity and the output level it forces are not stated in the pages read (only strobe current: 1.0/1.4 mA at 0.4 V).; Output level for open inputs not stated in the pages read.; Output stage type: the schematic shows an emitter-follower pull-up with resistor and a transistor pull-down; recorded as totem-pole.; The ND manual calls this part "Tri-state line receiver"; the DS8820 has no three-state output. The ND pinout (1 -, 2 terminate, 3 +, 4 strobe, 6 output) matches the DS8820 exactly.; Only a typical response time is printed; no tPLH/tPHL split and no maximum.
- **Notes:** Two independent receivers. ND symbol shows one half with pin numbers of the other half in brackets.

### 8831 - Line driver, single-ended and differential use

- **Part:** 8831
- **Description:** Line driver, single-ended and differential use
- **Formula:** OUT_x1 = IN_x1 XOR (MODE1 OR MODE2) when x_DIS1' AND x_DIS2'; Z otherwise; OUT_x2 = IN_x2 when x_DIS1' AND x_DIS2'; Z otherwise (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 52 (PDF page 58)

![8831 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/8831.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![8831 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-8831.png) | ![8831 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-8831.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1975 Interface Integrated Circuits, DS7831/DS8831, DS7832/DS8832 TRI-STATE line driver](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1975_National_Interface_Integrated_Circuits.pdf) p. pdf 110-113 (printed 3-37..3-40) (figures for DS8831)
- **Formulas:**
  - `OUT_x1 = IN_x1 XOR (MODE1 OR MODE2) when x_DIS1' AND x_DIS2'; Z otherwise` - Channel 1 of each pair passes its input straight through when both mode inputs are 0 and inverts it when either is 1 (differential mode). Floats when either disable input of its side is 1.
  - `OUT_x2 = IN_x2 when x_DIS1' AND x_DIS2'; Z otherwise` - Channel 2 always passes its input non-inverted; floats when either disable input of its side is 1. Tie IN_x1 to IN_x2 for a differential pair.
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `OUT_A1 = IN_A1 XOR (MODE1 OR MODE2)` (three-state) - drives when A_DIS1 low and A_DIS2 low, else Z
  - `OUT_A2 = IN_A2` (three-state) - drives when A_DIS1 low and A_DIS2 low, else Z
  - `OUT_B1 = IN_B1 XOR (MODE1 OR MODE2)` (three-state) - drives when B_DIS1 low and B_DIS2 low, else Z
  - `OUT_B2 = IN_B2` (three-state) - drives when B_DIS1 low and B_DIS2 low, else Z

| 1 | B_DIS1 | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; text: "B" output disable, NOR with pin 2; controls: 3, 5 |

| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

- **Electrical notes:** DS8831: VOL 0.5 V max at 40 mA (0.4 V at 32 mA); VOH 1.8 V min at -40 mA, 2.4 V min at -5.2 mA. Has VCC clamp diodes (DS8832 does not).
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** No minimum delays printed.; Load conditions for the switching table are on the waveform page (CL 50 pF / 5 pF jig) and were only partly legible.
- **Notes:** Mode pins 7 and 9 both feed one NOR gate shared by both sides.

### 8833 - Quad tri-state transceiver

- **Part:** 8833
- **Description:** Quad tri-state transceiver
- **Formula:** BUSx = INx when DRIVER_DISABLE'; Z when DRIVER_DISABLE high; OUTx = BUSx when RECEIVER_DISABLE'; Z when RECEIVER_DISABLE high (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 53 (PDF page 59)

![8833 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/8833.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![8833 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-8833.png) | ![8833 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-8833.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1975 Interface Integrated Circuits, DS7833/DS8833, DS7835/DS8835 quad TRI-STATE party line transceivers](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1975_National_Interface_Integrated_Circuits.pdf) p. pdf 115-118 (printed 3-42..3-45) (figures for DS8833)
- **Formulas:**
  - `BUSx = INx when DRIVER_DISABLE'; Z when DRIVER_DISABLE high` - The driver puts  INx on the bus while DRIVER DISABLE is low; floats while it is high.
  - `OUTx = BUSx when RECEIVER_DISABLE'; Z when RECEIVER_DISABLE high` - The receiver shows  the bus level while RECEIVER DISABLE is low; floats while it is high. Receiver thresholds (DS88xx): high 1.5..2.0 V, low 0.8..1.5 V, typ 1.75 / 1.35 V (hysteresis).
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `BUSA = INA` (three-state) - drives when DRIVER_DISABLE low, else Z
  - `OUTA = BUSA` (three-state) - drives when RECEIVER_DISABLE low, else Z
  - `BUSB = INB` (three-state) - drives when DRIVER_DISABLE low, else Z
  - `OUTB = BUSB` (three-state) - drives when RECEIVER_DISABLE low, else Z
  - `BUSC = INC` (three-state) - drives when DRIVER_DISABLE low, else Z
  - `OUTC = BUSC` (three-state) - drives when RECEIVER_DISABLE low, else Z
  - `BUSD = IND` (three-state) - drives when DRIVER_DISABLE low, else Z
  - `OUTD = BUSD` (three-state) - drives when RECEIVER_DISABLE low, else Z

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |

| 2 | INA | input | - |
| 3 | OUTA | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: RECEIVER_DISABLE |
| 4 | BUSB | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: DRIVER_DISABLE; text: bus pin: driver output and receiver input (with hysteresis) |

- **Output current:** low 50 mA, high -10.4 mA
- **Electrical notes:** Bus driver: sink 50 mA at 0.5 V (32 mA at 0.4 V), source -10.4 mA at 2.4 V (DS88xx). Receiver output: IOL 16 mA at 0.4 V, IOH -5.2 mA at 2.4 V. Bus pin leakage 80 uA max, also with VCC = 0 V.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** No minimum delays printed.; No truth table printed; function read from the description and connection diagram.; Driver Figure-1 / receiver Figure-2 load values were not legible in the text layer.
- **Notes:** BUS pins are driver outputs and receiver inputs at the same time.

### 8835 - Quad tri-state transceiver, inverting

- **Part:** 8835
- **Description:** Quad tri-state transceiver, inverting
- **Formula:** BUSx = INx' when DRIVER_DISABLE'; Z when DRIVER_DISABLE high; OUTx = BUSx' when RECEIVER_DISABLE'; Z when RECEIVER_DISABLE high (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 53 (PDF page 59)

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [National Semiconductor 1975 Interface Integrated Circuits, DS7833/DS8833, DS7835/DS8835 quad TRI-STATE party line transceivers](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/national/_dataBooks/1975_National_Interface_Integrated_Circuits.pdf) p. pdf 115-118 (printed 3-42..3-45) (figures for DS8835)
- **Formulas:**
  - `BUSx = INx' when DRIVER_DISABLE'; Z when DRIVER_DISABLE high` - The driver puts the inverse of INx on the bus while DRIVER DISABLE is low; floats while it is high.
  - `OUTx = BUSx' when RECEIVER_DISABLE'; Z when RECEIVER_DISABLE high` - The receiver shows the inverse of the bus level while RECEIVER DISABLE is low; floats while it is high. Receiver thresholds (DS88xx): high 1.5..2.0 V, low 0.8..1.5 V, typ 1.75 / 1.35 V (hysteresis).

  - `OUTA = NOT BUSA` (three-state) - drives when RECEIVER_DISABLE low, else Z

  - `OUTB = NOT BUSB` (three-state) - drives when RECEIVER_DISABLE low, else Z

  - `OUTC = NOT BUSC` (three-state) - drives when RECEIVER_DISABLE low, else Z
  - `BUSD = NOT IND` (three-state) - drives when DRIVER_DISABLE low, else Z
  - `OUTD = NOT BUSD` (three-state) - drives when RECEIVER_DISABLE low, else Z

### MC4007 - Dual 1-of-4 decoder

- **Part:** MC4007
- **Description:** Dual 1-of-4 decoder
- **Formula:** Q0 = NOT (E' AND X' AND Y'); Q1 = NOT (E' AND X AND Y'); Q2 = NOT (E' AND X' AND Y) ... (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 54 (PDF page 60)

![MC4007 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/MC4007.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![MC4007 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-MC4007.png) | ![MC4007 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-MC4007.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [Motorola 1971 TTL Integrated Circuits Data Book, MC4007L,P dual binary to one-of-four line decoder (MC4300/MC4000 series)](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/motorola/_dataBooks/1971_Motorola_TTL_Integrated_Circuits_Data_Book.pdf) p. pdf 229-230 (figures for MC4007L,P)
- **Formulas:**
  - `Q0 = NOT (E' AND X' AND Y')` - Q0 low when enabled and X=0, Y=0.
  - `Q1 = NOT (E' AND X AND Y')` - Q1 low when enabled and X=1, Y=0.
  - `Q2 = NOT (E' AND X' AND Y)` - Q2 low when enabled and X=0, Y=1.
  - `Q3 = NOT (E' AND X AND Y)` - Q3 low when enabled and X=1, Y=1. The formulas assume all outputs are high when E is high (see unknown).
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - `Q0_1 = NOT (E1' AND X1' AND Y1')` (totem-pole)
  - `Q1_1 = NOT (E1' AND X1 AND Y1')` (totem-pole)
  - `Q2_1 = NOT (E1' AND X1' AND Y1)` (totem-pole)
  - `Q3_1 = NOT (E1' AND X1 AND Y1)` (totem-pole)
  - `Q0_2 = NOT (E2' AND X2' AND Y2')` (totem-pole)
  - `Q1_2 = NOT (E2' AND X2 AND Y2')` (totem-pole)
  - `Q2_2 = NOT (E2' AND X2' AND Y2)` (totem-pole)
  - `Q3_2 = NOT (E2' AND X2 AND Y2)` (totem-pole)

| 2 | Q2_1 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 3 | Q1_1 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 4 | Q0_1 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 5 | E1 | enable | active: low; controls: 4, 3, 2, 1 |
| 6 | X1 | input | - |
| 7 | Y1 | input | - |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | Y2 | input | - |
| 10 | X2 | input | - |
| 11 | E2 | enable | active: low; controls: 12, 13, 14, 15 |
| 12 | Q0_2 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 13 | Q1_2 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 14 | Q2_2 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 15 | Q3_2 | output | outputType: totem-pole |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Output level with ENABLE high is not in the printed truth table (only E = 0 is shown); whenInactive left null.; Only typical delays are printed; no minimum or maximum.

Figures may differ from the parts ND used.; Switching times are typical only (the column could be typ; the sheet prints one number per row).; Minimum CTR read as 20% from the MCT2/MCT2E row; the text layer of that table is out of order.

### TMS6010 - Universal asynchronous receiver/transmitter (TMS 6011, AY-5-1012, AY-5-1013)

- **Part:** TMS6010
- **Description:** Universal asynchronous receiver/transmitter (TMS 6011, AY-5-1012, AY-5-1013)
- **Formula:** not printed in the manual; chip data not written yet
- **Manual page:** 59 (PDF page 65)

![TMS6010 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/TMS6010.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![TMS6010 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-TMS6010.png) | ![TMS6010 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-TMS6010.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [Texas Instruments 1975 The Semiconductor Memory Data Book, TMS 6011 JC, NC asynchronous data interface (UART), bulletin DL-S 7512275, May 1975](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/ti/_dataBooks/1975_TI_The_Semiconductor_Memory_Data_Book.pdf) p. pdf 153-158 (printed 152-157) (figures for TMS 6011)
- **Formulas:**
  - `-` - Received word, right-justified (RO1 = LSB); unused high bits low. Float while ROD is high. Not affected by MR.
  - `-` - PE high = parity error; forced low when PI is high. Floats while SFD is high.
  - `-` - FE high = framing error (missing or bad stop bit). Floats while SFD is high.
  - `-` - OE high = overrun (DR not reset before the next word arrived). Floats while SFD is high.
  - `-` - DR high = a word is in the receiver buffer register. DRR low resets it. Floats while SFD is high.
  - `-` - TBRE high = transmitter buffer register empty, ready for a new word. Floats while SFD is high.
  - `-` - TRE high = transmitter has sent a whole word including stop bits; stays high until the next word starts.
  - `-` - Serial out: start bit, 5-8 data bits, optional parity, 1 or 2 stop bits. High when idle; MR sets it high.
- **Stored state:** - bits, changes on TC / RC clocks (16 x baud)
  - MR high (after power-on): resets status and transmitter registers, TO high; RO not affected.
  - CRL high loads WLS1, WLS2, PS, PI, SBS into the control register.
  - Word length WLS1/WLS2: L/L 5, H/L 6, L/H 7, H/H 8 bits.
  - PS high = even parity, low = odd. PI high = no parity bit (PE forced low). SBS high = 2 stop bits, low = 1.
  - TBRL low loads TI1..TI8 into the transmitter buffer; TBRL low-to-high moves it into the transmitter register (delayed until the current word is sent).
- **Behaviour:** not written yet (see BUGS.md B3).

| 4 | ROD | enable | active: low; whenInactive: float; text: receiver output disable; controls: 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 11, 12 |
| 5 | RO8 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ROD |
| 6 | RO7 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ROD |
| 7 | RO6 | output | outputType: three-state; enabledBy: ROD |

### 3101 - 64-bit read/write memory (equal to 7489)

- **Part:** 3101
- **Description:** 64-bit read/write memory (equal to 7489)
- **Formula:** On = NOT mem[A3..A0][n] when CS' AND WE; else high (off) (datasheet)
- **Manual page:** 61 (PDF page 67)

![3101 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/3101.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![3101 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-3101.png) | ![3101 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-3101.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [Intel 1976 Data Catalog, 3101/3101A high speed fully decoded 64 bit memory](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/intel/_dataBooks/1976_Intel_Data_Catalog.pdf) p. pdf 97-99 (printed 2-87..2-89) (figures for 3101 (not 3101A))
- **Formulas:**
  - `On = NOT mem[A3..A0][n] when CS' AND WE; else high (off)` - Selected (CS low) and reading (WE high): outputs give the complement of the stored word. During a write, or when not selected, the outputs are high (open collector off).
- **Stored state:** 64 bits, changes on -
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - memory `M`: 16 words x 4 bits, address A0, A1, A2, A3
  - `O1 = NOT (NOT CS AND WE AND M1)` (open-collector)
  - `O2 = NOT (NOT CS AND WE AND M2)` (open-collector)
  - `O3 = NOT (NOT CS AND WE AND M3)` (open-collector)
  - `O4 = NOT (NOT CS AND WE AND M4)` (open-collector)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | A0 | input | - |
| 2 | CS | enable | active: low; whenInactive: high; controls: 5, 7, 9, 11 |
| 3 | WE | input | active: low |
| 4 | D1 | input | - |
| 5 | O1 | output | outputType: open-collector |
| 6 | D2 | input | - |
| 7 | O2 | output | outputType: open-collector |
| 8 | GND | ground | - |
| 9 | O3 | output | outputType: open-collector |
| 10 | D3 | input | - |
| 11 | O4 | output | outputType: open-collector |
| 12 | D4 | input | - |
| 13 | A3 | input | - |
| 14 | A2 | input | - |
| 15 | A1 | input | - |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

| From | To | Output edge | Min ns | Typ ns | Max ns | Conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A | O | access | 10 | - | 60 | VCC 5 V +-5%, TA 0-75 C, output load 15 mA and 30 pF, measured at 1.5 V |
| CS | O | access | 5 | - | 42 | VCC 5 V +-5%, TA 0-75 C, output load 15 mA and 30 pF, measured at 1.5 V |
| WE | O | recovery | - | - | 50 | VCC 5 V +-5%, TA 0-75 C, output load 15 mA and 30 pF, measured at 1.5 V |

- **Timing limits:** pulseWidth WE (tWP) minNs 40; setup D (data-write overlap tDW) to WE minNs 40; hold write recovery tWR to WE minNs 5
- **Output current:** low 15 mA, high - mA
- **Electrical notes:** VCC 5 V +-5%, TA 0-75 C. VOL 0.45 V at IOL 15 mA; output leakage 100 uA max at 5.25 V. VIL 0.85 V, VIH 2.0 V.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** Output pull-up voltage limit for the open collector is only the absolute maximum (-0.5..+7 V); outputMaxVoltageV left null.; Exact reference edges for tDW and tWR were taken from the parameter names; the write-cycle waveform was only partly legible.
- **Notes:** ND title says "equal to 7489". The National DM74L89A sheet (1976 National Memory Databook pdf 73) says pin compatible with SN7489, 3101, MM5501.

### 2532 - Quad 80-bit static shift register

- **Part:** 2532
- **Description:** Quad 80-bit static shift register
- **Formula:** not printed in the manual; chip data not written yet
- **Manual page:** 62 (PDF page 68)

![2532 as drawn in ND-13.003.01](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/2532.png)

| ND 1976 (redrawn from the cut-out) | Modern |
|---|---|
| ![2532 in ND style](../catalog/symbols/nd1976/shapes/block-2532.png) | ![2532 in modern style](../catalog/symbols/modern/shapes/block-2532.png) |

- **What it looks like:** A rectangle for the whole function (or one section of it). Pin numbers are written on the lines where they meet the box; signal names are written beside them.

- **Datasheet:** [Signetics 1972 MOS data book, 2532 quad 80-bit static shift register (ADVANCE SPECIFICATION)](https://www.bitsavers.org/components/signetics/_dataBooks/1972_Signetics_MOS.pdf) p. pdf 112-113 (printed 111-112) (figures for 2532B)
- **Formulas:**
  - `-` - OUTn is the last bit of the 80-bit register n. Each clock fall shifts the register one place.
- **Stored state:** 320 bits, changes on clock: data entered while PHI_IN is 1, shifted when it goes to 0
  - RECn = 0: INn is written into register n
  - RECn = 1: register n recirculates (output fed back), INn ignored
- **Behaviour** (as the simulator reads it):
  - 320 stored bits; the first two and the last:
  - stored `R1_1` (flip-flop): `R1_1 = (NOT REC1 AND IN1) OR (REC1 AND R1_80)` on falling edge (H->L) of PHI_IN
  - stored `R1_2` (flip-flop): `R1_2 = R1_1` on falling edge (H->L) of PHI_IN
  - stored `R4_80` (flip-flop): `R4_80 = R4_79` on falling edge (H->L) of PHI_IN
  - `OUT1 = R1_80` (totem-pole)
  - `OUT2 = R2_80` (totem-pole)
  - `OUT3 = R3_80` (totem-pole)
  - `OUT4 = R4_80` (totem-pole)

| Pin | Name | Role | Details |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | OUT1 | output | outputType: totem-pole; text: push-pull 0..+5 V |
| 2 | REC1 | input | text: recirculate: 1 = recirculate, 0 = load IN |
| 3 | IN1 | input | - |
| 4 | OUT2 | output | outputType: totem-pole; text: push-pull 0..+5 V |
| 5 | REC2 | input | text: recirculate: 1 = recirculate, 0 = load IN |
| 6 | IN2 | input | - |
| 7 | OUT3 | output | outputType: totem-pole; text: push-pull 0..+5 V |
| 8 | VDD | ground | text: VDD (ground) |
| 9 | REC3 | input | text: recirculate: 1 = recirculate, 0 = load IN |
| 10 | IN3 | input | - |
| 11 | PHI_IN | clock | edge: falling; text: single-phase clock common to all four registers; data entered while high, shifted when it goes low |
| 12 | VGG | power | voltage: -12 V |
| 13 | OUT4 | output | outputType: totem-pole; text: push-pull 0..+5 V |
| 14 | REC4 | input | text: recirculate: 1 = recirculate, 0 = load IN |
| 15 | IN4 | input | - |
| 16 | VCC | power | voltage: +5 V |

- **Output current:** low 1.6 mA, high - mA
- **Electrical notes:** Advance specification: sink 1.6 mA (one TTL load). Supplies +5 V and -12 V. DC to 2.5 MHz guaranteed. Inputs have internal pull-ups.
- **Not settled by the datasheet:** The datasheet read is an ADVANCE SPECIFICATION with no electrical or timing tables: propagation delay, clock widths, setup/hold and supply tolerances are all unknown.; Output source current not printed.
- **Notes:** Not the 2532 EPROM. Datasheet: pin-for-pin replacement for MK1007P and TMS3409.
